To further improve texture consistency during object movement, we propose a Test-Time Texture Consistency Optimization (TTCO) technique that adapts text and feature embeddings based on pixel correspondences from the simulator. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that PSIVG produces videos that better adhere to real-world physics while preserving visual quality and diversity. Project Page: https://vcai.mpi-inf.mpg.de/projects/PSIVG/

</details>


### [77] [RePer-360: Releasing Perspective Priors for 360$^\circ$ Depth Estimation via Self-Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05999)
*Cheng Guan,Chunyu Lin,Zhijie Shen,Junsong Zhang,Jiyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RePer-360是一个针对全景深度估计的轻量级自适应框架，通过几何对齐引导模块和自条件AdaLN-Zero机制，在保留预训练透视先验的同时，仅用1%数据就能实现比标准微调更好的全景域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度基础模型在透视图像上表现良好，但在360度全景图像上泛化能力差，因为透视和全景域之间存在显著的几何差异。完全微调这些模型通常需要大量全景数据，成本高昂。

Method: 提出RePer-360框架：1）轻量级几何对齐引导模块，从ERP和CP两种互补投影中提取调制信号，引导模型适应全景域而不覆盖预训练透视知识；2）自条件AdaLN-Zero机制，生成像素级缩放因子减少特征分布差距；3）立方体贴图域一致性损失，提高训练稳定性和跨投影对齐。

Result: RePer-360仅使用1%的训练数据就超越了标准微调方法。在相同域内训练设置下，RMSE提升了约20%。

Conclusion: 通过将重点从互补投影融合转向在保留预训练透视先验下的全景域适应，RePer-360实现了高效的全景深度估计，显著减少了数据需求并提升了性能。

Abstract: Recent depth foundation models trained on perspective imagery achieve strong performance, yet generalize poorly to 360$^\circ$ images due to the substantial geometric discrepancy between perspective and panoramic domains. Moreover, fully fine-tuning these models typically requires large amounts of panoramic data. To address this issue, we propose RePer-360, a distortion-aware self-modulation framework for monocular panoramic depth estimation that adapts depth foundation models while preserving powerful pretrained perspective priors. Specifically, we design a lightweight geometry-aligned guidance module to derive a modulation signal from two complementary projections (i.e., ERP and CP) and use it to guide the model toward the panoramic domain without overwriting its pretrained perspective knowledge. We further introduce a Self-Conditioned AdaLN-Zero mechanism that produces pixel-wise scaling factors to reduce the feature distribution gap between the perspective and panoramic domains. In addition, a cubemap-domain consistency loss further improves training stability and cross-projection alignment. By shifting the focus from complementary-projection fusion to panoramic domain adaptation under preserved pretrained perspective priors, RePer-360 surpasses standard fine-tuning methods while using only 1\% of the training data. Under the same in-domain training setting, it further achieves an approximately 20\% improvement in RMSE. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [78] [CLoPA: Continual Low Parameter Adaptation of Interactive Segmentation for Medical Image Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06426)
*Parhom Esmaeili,Chayanin Tangwiriyasakul,Eli Gibson,Sebastien Ourselin,M. Jorge Cardoso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLoPA是一种持续适应策略，通过在标注缓存上微调nnInteractive的少量参数，将零射交互分割模型的性能快速提升到专家水平，无需修改推理流程或增加新参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零射交互分割模型（如nnInteractive）在多样化医学影像任务中无法稳定达到专家级性能。由于标注活动会产生持续增长的特定任务标注数据流，在线适应分割模型成为零射推理的自然补充。

Method: 提出CLoPA持续适应策略，通过轻量级片段调度触发，在标注缓存上微调nnInteractive的少量参数。该方法不需要新参数或修改推理流程，完全在现有标注工作流中运行。

Result: 在涵盖多样化解剖目标和成像特征的八个Medical Segmentation Decathlon任务中，CLoPA快速将性能提升到专家水平，即使nnInteractive先前失败的任务也能成功。多数增益在单个训练片段后实现。不同参数组调优的效果取决于任务特征和数据状态。

Conclusion: CLoPA能有效提升交互分割模型的性能，但对于复杂几何结构（如肝血管）的目标，实例归一化和低层特征调优会饱和，表明在最具挑战性的场景中需要更深层的特征表示对齐。

Abstract: Interactive segmentation enables clinicians to guide annotation, but existing zero-shot models like nnInteractive fail to consistently reach expert-level performance across diverse medical imaging tasks. Because annotation campaigns produce a growing stream of task-specific labelled data, online adaptation of the segmentation model is a natural complement to zero-shot inference. We propose CLoPA, a continual adaptation strategy that tunes a small fraction of nnInteractive's parameters on the annotation cache, triggered by lightweight episode scheduling. CLoPA requires no new parameters or changes to the inference pipeline, and operates entirely within the existing annotation workflow. Across eight Medical Segmentation Decathlon tasks spanning diverse anatomical targets and imaging characteristics, CLoPA rapidly elevates performance to expert-level, even for tasks where nnInteractive previously failed, with the majority of gains realised after a single training episode. We show that the benefits of tuning different parameter groups depends on task characteristics and data regimes. Also, that for targets with complex geometries (e.g., hepatic vessels), instance normalisation and low-level feature tuning saturates, suggesting a need for deeper feature-representation alignment in the most challenging scenarios.

</details>


### [79] [Do Foundation Models Know Geometry? Probing Frozen Features for Continuous Physical Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06459)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型在几何信息编码上存在瓶颈：线性探针能从冻结特征中以6.1度MAE提取手部关节角度，而文本输出只能达到20.0度。训练目标比架构更重要，不同编码器在功能上收敛但表示不收敛。自回归生成会损害几何保真度，但损害来自生成过程而非语言对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视觉语言模型中视觉通路编码的几何信息与文本通路表达能力之间的差距，探索这种瓶颈的根源是表示能力不足还是训练缺陷，并寻找更高效的几何信息提取方法。

Method: 使用6,000参数的线性探针从冻结的视觉语言模型特征中提取手部关节角度；比较不同编码器架构（自监督、对比学习、混合范式）；进行LoRA微调实验；分析层间几何信号分布；评估自回归生成对几何保真度的影响。

Result: 线性探针能以6.1度MAE提取几何信息，而文本输出为20.0度（3.3倍差距）；LoRA微调可将差距缩小到6.5度；五种编码器在功能上收敛（R²≈0.55）但表示相似度低（CKA=0.41）；自回归生成损害几何保真度，但Qwen2.5-VL的LLM层实际上改善了探针精度；所有架构在中间层（18-22层）都有几何信号峰值。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型的几何信息瓶颈主要是训练缺陷而非表示能力问题；训练目标比架构更重要；可以通过轻量级探针从冻结骨干网络中提取多任务几何信息，无需微调或文本生成；自回归生成过程是几何保真度下降的主要原因。

Abstract: Vision-language models encode continuous geometry that their text pathway fails to express: a 6,000-parameter linear probe extracts hand joint angles at 6.1 degrees MAE from frozen features, while the best text output achieves only 20.0 degrees -- a 3.3x bottleneck. LoRA fine-tuning (r=16, 2,000 images) narrows this gap to 6.5 degrees, providing evidence for a pathway-training deficit rather than a representational one. Training objective determines accuracy more than architecture: five encoders spanning self-supervised, contrastive, and hybrid paradigms converge to statistically equivalent accuracy (R^2 approximately 0.55, TOST-equivalent at delta=0.03) despite sharing as little as CKA=0.41 representational similarity -- functional convergence without representational convergence. Autoregressive generation damages geometric fidelity, but the damage originates in the generation process, not in language alignment: Qwen2.5-VL's LLM layers actually improve probe accuracy over its raw vision encoder. Layer-wise analysis reveals a universal mid-network accuracy peak across all architectures, with attention heads in layers 18-22 carrying disproportionate geometric signal. These findings enable a single frozen backbone to function as a multi-task geometric sensor through lightweight probes, without fine-tuning or text generation.

</details>


### [80] [EffectMaker: Unifying Reasoning and Generation for Customized Visual Effect Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06014)
*Shiyuan Yang,Ruihuang Li,Jiale Tao,Shuai Shao,Qinglin Lu,Jing Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EffectMaker是一个统一的推理-生成框架，通过多模态大语言模型理解高级特效语义，结合扩散变换器进行上下文学习，实现无需逐特效微调的参考视频特效定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VFX生成面临数据稀缺、超自然/风格化特效建模困难、需要逐特效微调导致可扩展性和泛化性受限等问题，需要一种无需微调即可定制化生成高质量特效的解决方案。

Method: 采用语义-视觉双路径引导机制：1) 多模态大语言模型解释高级特效语义并推理如何适应目标主体；2) 扩散变换器通过上下文学习从参考视频中捕获细粒度视觉线索。构建了包含130k视频、3k特效类别的EffectData数据集。

Result: EffectMaker在视觉质量和特效一致性方面优于现有最先进基线，提供了一种可扩展且灵活的自定义VFX生成范式，无需逐特效微调。

Conclusion: EffectMaker通过统一的推理-生成框架和语义-视觉双路径引导，解决了VFX生成中的数据稀缺和泛化问题，为定制化特效生成提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual effects (VFX) are essential for enhancing the expressiveness and creativity of video content, yet producing high-quality effects typically requires expert knowledge and costly production pipelines. Existing AIGC systems face significant challenges in VFX generation due to the scarcity of effect-specific data and the inherent difficulty of modeling supernatural or stylized effects. Moreover, these approaches often require per-effect fine-tuning, which severely limits their scalability and generalization to novel VFX. In this work, we present EffectMaker, a unified reasoning-generation framework that enables reference-based VFX customization. EffectMaker employs a multimodal large language model to interpret high-level effect semantics and reason about how they should adapt to a target subject, while a diffusion transformer leverages in-context learning to capture fine-grained visual cues from reference videos. These two components form a semantic-visual dual-path guidance mechanism that enables accurate, controllable, and effect-consistent synthesis without per-effect fine-tuning. Furthermore, we construct EffectData, the largest high-quality synthetic dataset containing 130k videos across 3k VFX categories, to improve generalization and scalability. Experiments show that EffectMaker achieves superior visual quality and effect consistency over state-of-the-art baselines, offering a scalable and flexible paradigm for customized VFX generation. Project page: https://effectmaker.github.io

</details>


### [81] [Artificial Intelligence for Detecting Fetal Orofacial Clefts and Advancing Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06522)
*Yuanji Zhang,Yuhao Huang,Haoran Dou,Xiliang Zhu,Chen Ling,Zhong Yang,Lianying Liang,Jiuping Li,Siying Liang,Rui Li,Yan Cao,Yuhan Zhang,Jiewei Lai,Yongsong Zhou,Hongyu Zheng,Xinru Gao,Cheng Yu,Liling Shi,Mengqin Yuan,Honglong Li,Xiaoqiong Huang,Chaoyu Chen,Jialin Zhang,Wenxiong Pan,Alejandro F. Frangi,Guangzhi He,Xin Yang,Yi Xiong,Linliang Yin,Xuedong Deng,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AI系统在超声图像上诊断胎儿口面裂，准确率超过93%，媲美资深放射科医生，并能作为医疗副驾驶提升初级医生诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口面裂是最常见的先天性颅面异常之一，但产前准确检测具有挑战性，因为经验丰富的专家稀缺且该病症相对罕见。早期可靠诊断对于及时临床干预和降低相关发病率至关重要。

Method: 开发了一个人工智能系统，使用来自22家医院9,215名胎儿的45,139张超声图像进行训练，用于诊断胎儿口面裂。该系统可作为医疗副驾驶辅助放射科医生。

Result: AI系统诊断胎儿口面裂的敏感性和特异性分别超过93%和95%，与资深放射科医生表现相当，并显著优于初级放射科医生。作为医疗副使用时，能将初级放射科医生的敏感性提高6%以上。涉及24名放射科医生和学员的试点研究表明，该模型能改善罕见病症的专业知识发展。

Conclusion: 这种双重用途方法为在经验丰富的放射科医生稀缺的环境中提高诊断准确性和专家培训提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Orofacial clefts are among the most common congenital craniofacial abnormalities, yet accurate prenatal detection remains challenging due to the scarcity of experienced specialists and the relative rarity of the condition. Early and reliable diagnosis is essential to enable timely clinical intervention and reduce associated morbidity. Here we show that an artificial intelligence system, trained on over 45,139 ultrasound images from 9,215 fetuses across 22 hospitals, can diagnose fetal orofacial clefts with sensitivity and specificity exceeding 93% and 95% respectively, matching the performance of senior radiologists and substantially outperforming junior radiologists. When used as a medical copilot, the system raises junior radiologists' sensitivity by more than 6%. Beyond direct diagnostic assistance, the system also accelerates the development of clinical expertise. A pilot study involving 24 radiologists and trainees demonstrated that the model can improve the expertise development for rare conditions. This dual-purpose approach offers a scalable solution for improving both diagnostic accuracy and specialist training in settings where experienced radiologists are scarce.

</details>


### [82] [MOSIV: Multi-Object System Identification from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06022)
*Chunjiang Liu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Qingran Lin,Albert Xiao,Haoyu Chen,Shizheng Wen,Hao Zhang,Lu Qi,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Laszlo A. Jeni,Min Xu,Yizhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOSIV框架，用于从视频中进行多物体系统识别，通过可微分模拟器直接优化连续、每物体的材料参数，显著提升多物体交互场景的识别精度和模拟保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要针对单物体场景或使用固定材料原型的离散分类，无法处理多物体系统识别这一挑战性问题，特别是需要连续材料参数和复杂物体交互的场景。

Method: 提出MOSIV框架，使用可微分模拟器直接优化连续、每物体的材料参数，通过从视频中提取的几何目标进行指导，并创建了包含接触丰富的多物体交互的合成基准数据集。

Result: 在新建的基准测试中，MOSIV相比适应后的基线方法，显著提高了接地精度和长时程模拟保真度，成为该新任务的强基线方法。

Conclusion: 物体级细粒度监督和几何对齐的目标对于复杂多物体设置中的稳定优化至关重要，MOSIV为多物体系统识别提供了有效的解决方案，代码和数据集将开源。

Abstract: We introduce the challenging problem of multi-object system identification from videos, for which prior methods are ill-suited due to their focus on single-object scenes or discrete material classification with a fixed set of material prototypes. To address this, we propose MOSIV, a new framework that directly optimizes for continuous, per-object material parameters using a differentiable simulator guided by geometric objectives derived from video. We also present a new synthetic benchmark with contact-rich, multi-object interactions to facilitate evaluation. On this benchmark, MOSIV substantially improves grounding accuracy and long-horizon simulation fidelity over adapted baselines, establishing it as a strong baseline for this new task. Our analysis shows that object-level fine-grained supervision and geometry-aligned objectives are critical for stable optimization in these complex, multi-object settings. The source code and dataset will be released.

</details>


### [83] [SUREON: A Benchmark and Vision-Language-Model for Surgical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06570)
*Alejandra Perez,Anita Rau,Lee White,Busisiwe Mlambo,Chinedu Nwoye,Muhammad Abdullah Jamal,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUREON是一个大规模外科视频问答数据集，通过从外科教学视频中提取专家讲解来训练AI理解外科推理过程，包括安全评估、决策理由和预测等12类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前外科AI缺乏理解外科推理的能力，因为大规模标注包含外科推理的训练数据非常困难。然而外科教学视频中已经包含了专家讲解的意图、理由和预测等推理内容。

Method: 从外科学术视频中系统性地提取训练信号，定义12个问题类别，使用多智能体管道大规模提取和结构化监督数据。开发了两个模型：SureonVLM（通过监督微调）和SureonVLM-R1（使用Group Relative Policy Optimization训练）。

Result: 构建了包含134.7K个视频片段、170种手术类型、206.8k个问答对的数据集，以及354个专家验证的基准测试。两个模型都能回答复杂的外科问题，在SUREON基准上准确率超过84%，在标准外科感知任务上优于更大的通用领域模型。

Conclusion: 通过从外科教学视频中提取专家讲解，可以有效地训练AI理解外科推理过程，填补了当前外科AI在理解意图、理由和预测方面的能力空白。

Abstract: Surgeons don't just see -- they interpret. When an expert observes a surgical scene, they understand not only what instrument is being used, but why it was chosen, what risk it poses, and what comes next. Current surgical AI cannot answer such questions, largely because training data that explicitly encodes surgical reasoning is immensely difficult to annotate at scale. Yet surgical video lectures already contain exactly this -- explanations of intent, rationale, and anticipation, narrated by experts for the purpose of teaching. Though inherently noisy and unstructured, these narrations encode the reasoning that surgical AI currently lacks. We introduce SUREON, a large-scale video QA dataset that systematically harvests this training signal from surgical academic videos. SUREON defines 12 question categories covering safety assessment, decision rationale, and forecasting, and uses a multi-agent pipeline to extract and structure supervision at scale. Across 134.7K clips and 170 procedure types, SUREON yields 206.8k QA pairs and an expert-validated benchmark of 354 examples. To evaluate the extent to which this supervision translates to surgical reasoning ability, we introduce two models: SureonVLM, a vision-language model adapted through supervised fine-tuning, and SureonVLM-R1, a reasoning model trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization. Both models can answer complex questions about surgery and substantially outperform larger general-domain models, exceeding 84% accuracy on the SUREON benchmark while outperforming general-domain models on standard surgical perception tasks. Qualitative analysis of SureonVLM-R1 reveals explicit reasoning behavior, such as inferring operative intent from visual context.

</details>


### [84] [StruVis: Enhancing Reasoning-based Text-to-Image Generation via Thinking with Structured Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06032)
*Yuanhuiyi Lyu,Kaiyu Lei,Ziqiao Weng,Xu Zheng,Lutao Jiang,Teng Li,Yangfu Li,Ziyuan Huang,Linfeng Zhang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StruVis提出了一种基于结构化视觉表示的新型文本到图像生成推理框架，通过文本形式的结构化视觉表示作为中间推理状态，避免了传统方法的视觉缺失或计算成本过高问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成的推理框架存在两个主要问题：1）纯文本推理虽然计算高效但缺乏视觉上下文，容易遗漏关键空间和视觉元素；2）文本-图像交错推理虽然能提供视觉参考，但计算成本高且受限于生成器的表示能力。

Method: 提出StruVis框架，采用基于文本的结构化视觉表示作为中间推理状态，让多模态大语言模型在纯文本推理过程中有效"感知"视觉结构，实现结构化视觉引导的推理，且框架与生成器无关，可无缝集成多种文本到图像生成器。

Result: 实验表明StruVis在推理型文本到图像生成基准上取得显著性能提升，如在T2I-ReasonBench上获得4.61%增益，在WISE上获得4%增益。

Conclusion: StruVis通过结构化视觉表示有效解决了现有推理框架的局限性，在保持计算效率的同时提升了视觉准确性，为推理型文本到图像生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning-based text-to-image (T2I) generation requires models to interpret complex prompts accurately. Existing reasoning frameworks can be broadly categorized into two types: (1) Text-Only Reasoning, which is computationally efficient but lacks access to visual context, often resulting in the omission of critical spatial and visual elements; and (2) Text-Image Interleaved Reasoning, which leverages a T2I generator to provide visual references during the reasoning process. While this approach enhances visual grounding, it incurs substantial computational costs and constrains the reasoning capacity of MLLMs to the representational limitations of the generator. To this end, we propose StruVis, a novel framework that enhances T2I generation through Thinking with Structured Vision. Instead of relying on intermediate image generation, StruVis employs text-based structured visual representations as intermediate reasoning states, thereby enabling the MLLM to effectively "perceive" visual structure within a purely text-based reasoning process. Powered by this, the reasoning potential for T2I generation of the MLLM is unlocked through structured-vision-guided reasoning. Additionally, as a generator-agnostic reasoning framework, our proposed StruVis can be seamlessly integrated with diverse T2I generators and efficiently enhance their performance in reasoning-based T2I generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StruVis achieves significant performance improvements on reasoning-based T2I benchmarks, e.g., a 4.61% gain on T2I-ReasonBench and a 4% gain on WISE.

</details>


### [85] [Occlusion-Aware SORT: Observing Occlusion for Robust Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06034)
*Chunjiang Li,Jianbo Ma,Li Shen,Yanru Chen,Liangyin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OA-SORT是一个无需训练的即插即用多目标跟踪框架，通过遮挡感知模块、偏移和动量机制解决2D MOT中的遮挡问题，在多个数据集上显著提升跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D多目标跟踪面临部分遮挡导致的定位成本混淆问题，现有方法在处理遮挡时效果有限，需要更有效的遮挡感知机制来提升跟踪准确性。

Method: 提出OA-SORT框架，包含三个核心组件：1)遮挡感知模块(OAM)分析物体遮挡状态，引入高斯图减少背景影响；2)遮挡感知偏移(OAO)利用OAM描述的遮挡状态缓解成本混淆；3)偏差感知动量(BAM)抑制估计不稳定性。

Result: 在DanceTrack测试集上达到63.1% HOTA和64.2% IDF1；将遮挡感知框架集成到其他四个跟踪器后，HOTA和IDF1平均提升2.08%和3.05%，证明了框架的可重用性。

Conclusion: 遮挡处理对多目标跟踪至关重要，OA-SORT框架通过创新的遮挡感知机制有效解决了遮挡引起的跟踪问题，具有即插即用、无需训练的优点，且可迁移到其他跟踪器中提升性能。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) involves analyzing object trajectories and counting the number of objects in video sequences. However, 2D MOT faces challenges due to positional cost confusion arising from partial occlusion. To address this issue, we present the novel Occlusion-Aware SORT (OA-SORT) framework, a plug-and-play and training-free framework that includes the Occlusion-Aware Module (OAM), the Occlusion-Aware Offset (OAO), and the Bias-Aware Momentum (BAM). Specifically, OAM analyzes the occlusion status of objects, where a Gaussian Map (GM) is introduced to reduce background influence. In contrast, OAO and BAM leverage the OAM-described occlusion status to mitigate cost confusion and suppress estimation instability. Comprehensive evaluations on the DanceTrack, SportsMOT, and MOT17 datasets demonstrate the importance of occlusion handling in MOT. On the DanceTrack test set, OA-SORT achieves 63.1% and 64.2% in HOTA and IDF1, respectively. Furthermore, integrating the Occlusion-Aware framework into the four additional trackers improves HOTA and IDF1 by an average of 2.08% and 3.05%, demonstrating the reusability of the occlusion awareness.

</details>


### [86] [BEVLM: Distilling Semantic Knowledge from LLMs into Bird's-Eye View Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06576)
*Thomas Monninger,Shaoyuan Xie,Qi Alfred Chen,Sihao Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEVLM框架将空间一致的BEV表示与LLMs连接，提升自动驾驶中的3D空间推理和语义理解能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多视角多帧图像独立输入LLMs，导致计算冗余和空间一致性不足，而BEV表示虽有空间结构但缺乏语义丰富性，需要桥接这一鸿沟

Method: 提出BEVLM框架，将空间一致且语义蒸馏的BEV表示与LLMs连接，利用BEV特征作为统一输入，并将LLMs的语义知识蒸馏到BEV表示中

Result: BEVLM使LLMs在跨视角驾驶场景中的推理准确率提升46%，通过语义知识蒸馏到BEV表示，在安全关键场景中的端到端闭环驾驶性能提升29%

Conclusion: BEVLM成功桥接了空间一致的BEV表示与语义丰富的LLMs，显著提升了自动驾驶系统的3D空间推理能力和端到端驾驶性能

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into autonomous driving has attracted growing interest for their strong reasoning and semantic understanding abilities, which are essential for handling complex decision-making and long-tail scenarios. However, existing methods typically feed LLMs with tokens from multi-view and multi-frame images independently, leading to redundant computation and limited spatial consistency. This separation in visual processing hinders accurate 3D spatial reasoning and fails to maintain geometric coherence across views. On the other hand, Bird's-Eye View (BEV) representations learned from geometrically annotated tasks (e.g., object detection) provide spatial structure but lack the semantic richness of foundation vision encoders. To bridge this gap, we propose BEVLM, a framework that connects a spatially consistent and semantically distilled BEV representation with LLMs. Through extensive experiments, we show that BEVLM enables LLMs to reason more effectively in cross-view driving scenes, improving accuracy by 46%, by leveraging BEV features as unified inputs. Furthermore, by distilling semantic knowledge from LLMs into BEV representations, BEVLM significantly improves closed-loop end-to-end driving performance by 29% in safety-critical scenarios.

</details>


### [87] [Ensemble Learning with Sparse Hypercolumns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06036)
*Julia Dietlmeier,Vayangi Ganepola,Oluwabukola G. Adegboro,Mayug Maniparambil,Claudia Mazo,Noel E. O'Connor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用分层子采样处理VGG16超列特征，并通过集成学习方法在脑肿瘤分割任务上取得显著性能提升，特别是在小样本情况下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超列特征虽然受生物视觉启发，能有效用于图像分割，但计算复杂度随训练集大小线性增长，限制了实际应用。需要解决高维超列特征处理的计算效率问题。

Method: 采用分层子采样策略处理VGG16生成的超列特征，减少计算复杂度。研究堆叠和投票等集成学习方法在稀疏超列上的性能表现。

Result: 在脑肿瘤数据集上，当N≤20的极端小样本情况下，简单逻辑回归分类器表现最佳。在10%分层子采样率下，N=20时最佳平均Dice得分为0.66，相比标准多尺度UNet基线有24.53%的显著提升（p值=3.07e-11）。

Conclusion: 分层子采样能有效降低超列特征的计算复杂度，集成学习方法在小样本脑肿瘤分割任务中表现优异，特别是在极端小样本情况下，简单分类器比复杂模型更有效。

Abstract: Directly inspired by findings in biological vision, high-dimensional hypercolumns are feature vectors built by concatenating multi-scale activations of convolutional neural networks for a single image pixel location. Together with powerful classifiers, they can be used for image segmentation i.e. pixel classification. However, in practice, there are only very few works dedicated to the use of hypercolumns. One reason is the computational complexity of processing concatenated dense hypercolumns that grows linearly with the size $N$ of the training set. In this work, we address this challenge by applying stratified subsampling to the VGG16 based hypercolumns. Furthermore, we investigate the performance of ensemble learning on sparse hypercolumns. Our experiments on a brain tumor dataset show that stacking and voting ensembles deliver competitive performance, but in the extreme low-shot case of $N \leq 20$, a simple Logistic Regression classifier is the most effective method. For 10% stratified subsampling rate, our best average Dice score is 0.66 for $N=20$. This is a statistically significant improvement of 24.53% over the standard multi-scale UNet baseline ($p$-value = $[3.07e-11]$, Wilcoxon signed-rank test), which is less effective due to overfitting.

</details>


### [88] [FontUse: A Data-Centric Approach to Style- and Use-Case-Conditioned In-Image Typography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06038)
*Xia Xin,Yuki Endo,Yoshihiro Kanamori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FontUse数据集和标注流程，通过数据驱动方法改进文本到图像模型中的字体排版控制，使模型能更好地遵循字体样式和使用场景的提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到图像模型在字体排版控制方面存在不足，用户请求的字体样式经常被忽略或弱化执行，需要专门的数据集和方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 构建FontUse大规模字体数据集（约70K图像），使用分割模型和多模态大语言模型自动标注用户友好提示、文本区域位置和OCR识别字符串；通过微调现有生成模型，使其能一致地解释样式和使用场景条件。

Result: 实验表明，使用该流程训练的模型在多样化的提示和布局下，生成的文本渲染比竞争基线更符合提示要求；提出了基于Long-CLIP的评估指标来衡量字体属性对齐度。

Conclusion: 通过数据中心的标注流程构建FontUse数据集，有效提升了文本到图像模型对字体排版的细粒度控制能力，使模型能更好地遵循字体样式和使用场景的提示。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image models can generate high-quality images from natural-language prompts, yet controlling typography remains challenging: requested typographic appearance is often ignored or only weakly followed. We address this limitation with a data-centric approach that trains image generation models using targeted supervision derived from a structured annotation pipeline specialized for typography. Our pipeline constructs a large-scale typography-focused dataset, FontUse, consisting of about 70K images annotated with user-friendly prompts, text-region locations, and OCR-recognized strings. The annotations are automatically produced using segmentation models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). The prompts explicitly combine font styles (e.g., serif, script, elegant) and use cases (e.g., wedding invitations, coffee-shop menus), enabling intuitive specification even for novice users. Fine-tuning existing generators with these annotations allows them to consistently interpret style and use-case conditions as textual prompts without architectural modification. For evaluation, we introduce a Long-CLIP-based metric that measures alignment between generated typography and requested attributes. Experiments across diverse prompts and layouts show that models trained with our pipeline produce text renderings more consistent with prompts than competitive baselines. The source code for our annotation pipeline is available at https://github.com/xiaxinz/FontUSE.

</details>


### [89] [Devil is in Narrow Policy: Unleashing Exploration in Driving VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06049)
*Canyu Chen,Yuguang Yang,Zhewen Tan,Yizhi Wang,Ruiyi Zhan,Haiyan Liu,Xuanyao Mao,Jason Bao,Xinyue Tang,Linlin Yang,Bingchuan Sun,Yan Wang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Curious-VLA框架通过两阶段设计解决VLA模型中的探索-利用困境：IL阶段使用可行轨迹扩展和归一化表示增加数据多样性，RL阶段采用自适应多样性感知采样和跨度驾驶奖励来提升驾驶质量敏感性，在Navsim基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶VLA模型存在窄策略限制问题：模仿学习倾向于收敛探索范围，限制了后续强化学习阶段的潜力，导致过早饱和，主要原因是反馈多样性不足。

Method: 提出Curious-VLA两阶段框架：IL阶段使用可行轨迹扩展策略生成多个物理有效轨迹，采用步进归一化轨迹表示适应多样化数据；RL阶段引入自适应多样性感知采样优先选择高多样性样本，提出跨度驾驶奖励和焦点风格加权来扩大奖励值跨度。

Result: 在Navsim基准测试中取得SOTA结果：PDMS 90.3，EPDMS 85.4，Best-of-N PDMS达到94.8，有效释放了VLA模型的探索潜力。

Conclusion: Curious-VLA通过解决VLA模型中的探索-利用困境，显著提升了自动驾驶模型的性能，证明了多样化数据生成和奖励设计对于解锁VLA模型探索潜力的重要性。

Abstract: We identify a fundamental Narrow Policy limitation undermining the performance of autonomous VLA models, where driving Imitation Learning (IL) tends to collapse exploration and limit the potential of subsequent Reinforcement Learning (RL) stages, which often saturate prematurely due to insufficient feedback diversity. Thereby, we propose Curious-VLA, a framework that alleviates the exploit-explore dilemma through a two-stage design. During IL, we introduce a Feasible Trajectory Expansion (FTE) strategy to generate multiple physically valid trajectories and a step-wise normalized trajectory representation to adapt this diverse data. In the RL stage, we present Adaptive Diversity-Aware Sampling (ADAS) that prioritizes high-diversity samples and introduce Spanning Driving Reward (SDR) with a focal style weighting to amplify reward's value span for improving sensitivity to driving quality. On the Navsim benchmark, Curious-VLA achieves SoTA results (PDMS 90.3, EPDMS 85.4) and a Best-of-N PDMS of 94.8, demonstrating its effectiveness in unlocking the exploratory potential of VLA models. Code: https://github.com/Mashiroln/curious_vla.git.

</details>


### [90] [Learning to Generate via Understanding: Understanding-Driven Intrinsic Rewarding for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06043)
*Jiadong Pan,Liang Li,Yuxin Peng,Yu-Ming Tang,Shuohuan Wang,Yu Sun,Hua Wu,Qingming Huang,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GvU框架，通过理解分支的内在奖励机制增强统一多模态模型的生成能力，缩小理解与生成之间的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型在视觉理解方面表现出色，但生成能力相对较弱，存在理解与生成过程的内在解耦问题。模型能准确理解细粒度视觉细节，却难以从复杂文本提示生成语义连贯的图像。

Method: 提出GvU框架：1）基于理解分支设计token级内在文本-图像对齐奖励机制；2）让模型同时作为教师和学生，用理解分支评估自身输出并指导生成；3）构建自监督强化学习框架，通过理解奖励信号迭代提升生成质量。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著提升了统一多模态模型的生成能力，同时增强了细粒度视觉理解，缩小了视觉理解与生成之间的能力差距。

Conclusion: 通过利用模型内部理解能力来指导生成过程，可以有效缩小统一多模态模型中理解与生成的能力差距，实现两者协同提升。

Abstract: Recently, unified multimodal models (UMMs) have made remarkable progress in integrating visual understanding and generation, demonstrating strong potential for complex text-to-image (T2I) tasks. Despite their theoretical promise, a persistent capability gap exists: UMMs typically exhibit superior visual understanding but comparatively weaker generative capabilities. This discrepancy arises largely from the intrinsic decoupling between the understanding and generation processes. While a UMM can accurately interpret fine-grained visual details, it often struggles to produce semantically coherent images from complex textual prompts. To address this challenge, we explore UMMs' internal understanding capability to enhance generation quality. We propose a token-level intrinsic text-image alignment reward mechanism, GvU, enabling the UMM to act simultaneously as teacher and student: it evaluates its own outputs using the understanding branch to guide the generations accordingly. Building upon this, we design a self-supervised reinforcement learning framework, allowing UMMs to iteratively improve their generation quality through understanding-based intrinsic reward signals--without reliance on external supervision. Experimental results show that our method substantially boosts UMMs' generation, which in turn strengthens their fine-grained visual understanding, narrowing the capability gap between UMMs' visual understanding and generation.

</details>


### [91] [Transforming Omnidirectional RGB-LiDAR data into 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06061)
*Semin Bae,Hansol Lim,Jongseong Brad Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个从存档的RGB-LiDAR日志中创建3D高斯溅射数字孪生的确定性工作流，通过多模态融合解决传统方法中的跟踪和计算瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学和自动驾驶领域对大规模数字孪生的需求快速增长，但传统3DGS构建需要昂贵的专用数据采集。同时，已部署平台收集的大量全向RGB和LiDAR日志由于传输限制和缺乏可扩展重用管道而被大量浪费。

Method: 提出全向RGB-LiDAR重用管道：1) ERP到立方体贴图转换模块实现确定性空间锚定；2) PRISM颜色分层下采样策略；3) 基于FPFH的全局配准和ICP算法桥接多模态输入；4) LiDAR增强的初始化提升3DGS渲染质量。

Result: 成功将大量被丢弃的数据转化为可用的SfM几何，在结构复杂场景中，LiDAR增强的初始化相比纯视觉基线持续提升了最终3DGS渲染保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作为从标准存档传感器日志创建仿真级数字孪生提供了确定性工作流，有效解决了现有数据利用不足的问题。

Abstract: The demand for large-scale digital twins is rapidly growing in robotics and autonomous driving. However, constructing these environments with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) usually requires expensive, purpose-built data collection. Meanwhile, deployed platforms routinely collect extensive omnidirectional RGB and LiDAR logs, but a significant portion of these sensor data is directly discarded or strictly underutilized due to transmission constraints and the lack of scalable reuse pipeline. In this paper, we present an omnidirectional RGB-LiDAR reuse pipeline that transforms these archived logs into robust initialization assets for 3DGS. Direct conversion of such raw logs introduces practical bottlenecks: inherent non-linear distortion leads to unreliable Structure-from-Motion (SfM) tracking, and dense, unorganized LiDAR clouds cause computational overhead during 3DGS optimization. To overcome these challenges, our pipeline strategically integrates an ERP-to-cubemap conversion module for deterministic spatial anchoring, alongside PRISM-a color stratified downsampling strategy. By bridging these multi-modal inputs via Fast Point Feature Histograms (FPFH) based global registration and Iterative Closest Point (ICP), our pipeline successfully repurposes a considerable fraction of discarded data into usable SfM geometry. Furthermore, our LiDAR-reinforced initialization consistently enhances the final 3DGS rendering fidelity in structurally complex scenes compared to vision-only baselines. Ultimately, this work provides a deterministic workflow for creating simulation-grade digital twins from standard archived sensor logs.

</details>


### [92] [GenHOI: Towards Object-Consistent Hand-Object Interaction with Temporally Balanced and Spatially Selective Object Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06048)
*Xuan Huang,Mochu Xiang,Zhelun Shen,Jinbo Wu,Chenming Wu,Chen Zhao,Kaisiyuan Wang,Hang Zhou,Shanshan Liu,Haocheng Feng,Wei He,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenHOI是一个轻量级增强模块，通过时间平衡和空间选择的方式向预训练视频生成模型注入参考物体信息，显著提升手-物体交互视频合成的物理合理性和物体一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前手-物体交互视频合成存在两个主要问题：1）专门的手-物体交互重演方法通常在域内训练和评估，难以泛化到复杂的真实场景；2）通用视频编辑模型虽然鲁棒性更强，但仍存在物体外观不一致等手-物体交互特定问题。

Method: 提出GenHOI方法，包含两个关键技术：1）Head-Sliding RoPE：为参考token分配特定时间偏移，平衡参考信息在时间上的影响，缓解3D RoPE的时间衰减问题；2）两级空间注意力门：将物体条件注意力集中在手-物体交互区域，并自适应调整强度，在保持背景真实性的同时增强交互保真度。

Result: 在未见过的真实场景上进行广泛定性和定量评估，GenHOI在手-物体交互重演和通用视频编辑方法中均显著优于现有最佳方法。

Conclusion: GenHOI通过轻量级增强预训练视频生成模型，有效解决了手-物体交互视频合成中的物体一致性和物理合理性问题，在复杂真实场景中表现出色。

Abstract: Hand-Object Interaction (HOI) remains a core challenge in digital human video synthesis, where models must generate physically plausible contact and preserve object identity across frames. Although recent HOI reenactment approaches have achieved progress, they are typically trained and evaluated in-domain and fail to generalize to complex, in-the-wild scenarios. In contrast, all-in-one video editing models exhibit broader robustness but still struggle with HOI-specific issues such as inconsistent object appearance. In this paper, we present GenHOI, a lightweight augmentation to pretrained video generation models that injects reference-object information in a temporally balanced and spatially selective manner. For temporal balancing, we propose Head-Sliding RoPE, which assigns head-specific temporal offsets to reference tokens, distributing their influence evenly across frames and mitigating the temporal decay of 3D RoPE to improve long-range object consistency. For spatial selectivity, we design a two-level spatial attention gate that concentrates object-conditioned attention on HOI regions and adaptively scales its strength, preserving background realism while enhancing interaction fidelity. Extensive qualitative and quantitative evaluations on unseen, in-the-wild scenes demonstrate that GenHOI significantly outperforms state-of-the-art HOI reenactment and all-in-one video editing methods. Project page: https://xuanhuang0.github.io/GenHOI/

</details>


### [93] [VG3S: Visual Geometry Grounded Gaussian Splatting for Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06210)
*Xiaoyang Yan,Muleilan Pei,Shaojie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VG3S是一个利用视觉基础模型(VFMs)的几何先验来增强3D高斯泼溅占用预测的新框架，通过在nuScenes基准上显著提升IoU和mIoU指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于3D高斯泼溅的占用预测方法严重依赖准确的几何线索，但在纯视觉范式中这些线索往往不足。需要将视觉基础模型强大的几何感知能力注入到占用预测中。

Method: 提出VG3S框架，包含一个即插即用的分层几何特征适配器，通过特征聚合、任务特定对齐和多尺度重构来有效转换通用的VFM特征。

Result: 在nuScenes占用基准测试中，VG3S相比基线在IoU上提升了12.6%，在mIoU上提升了7.5%。该框架能无缝泛化到不同的VFMs，一致提升占用预测精度。

Conclusion: VG3S证明了将预训练的几何感知视觉基础模型的先验知识整合到高斯泼溅占用预测中的巨大价值，为自动驾驶场景理解提供了更强大的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction has become a crucial perception task for comprehensive scene understanding in autonomous driving. While recent advances have explored 3D Gaussian splatting for occupancy modeling to substantially reduce computational overhead, the generation of high-quality 3D Gaussians relies heavily on accurate geometric cues, which are often insufficient in purely vision-centric paradigms. To bridge this gap, we advocate for injecting the strong geometric grounding capability from Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) into occupancy prediction. In this regard, we introduce Visual Geometry Grounded Gaussian Splatting (VG3S), a novel framework that empowers Gaussian-based occupancy prediction with cross-view 3D geometric grounding. Specifically, to fully exploit the rich 3D geometric priors from a frozen VFM, we propose a plug-and-play hierarchical geometric feature adapter, which can effectively transform generic VFM tokens via feature aggregation, task-specific alignment, and multi-scale restructuring. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes occupancy benchmark demonstrate that VG3S achieves remarkable improvements of 12.6% in IoU and 7.5% in mIoU over the baseline. Furthermore, we show that VG3S generalizes seamlessly across diverse VFMs, consistently enhancing occupancy prediction accuracy and firmly underscoring the immense value of integrating priors derived from powerful, pre-trained geometry-grounded VFMs.

</details>


### [94] [NOVA: Next-step Open-Vocabulary Autoregression for 3D Multi-Object Tracking in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06254)
*Kai Luo,Xu Wang,Rui Fan,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOVA提出了一种基于大语言模型自回归的开放词汇3D多目标跟踪新范式，将轨迹建模为时空语义序列，显著提升未知类别跟踪性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D多目标跟踪方法受限于闭集假设和"语义盲"启发式规则，无法泛化到未知目标。需要一种能够处理开放世界感知的新方法。

Method: 将3D轨迹重新定义为结构化时空语义序列，利用大语言模型的自回归能力，将跟踪任务转化为原则性的下一步序列补全过程，同时编码物理运动连续性和深度语言先验。

Result: 在nuScenes、V2X-Seq-SPD和KITTI数据集上表现出优越性能。在nuScenes数据集上，NOVA在Novel类别上达到22.41%的AMOTA，比基线绝对提升20.21%，仅使用0.5B参数的自回归模型。

Conclusion: NOVA通过将3D跟踪从传统的基于距离的匹配转向生成式时空语义建模，成功解决了开放词汇跟踪问题，利用语言空间层次结构解决语义歧义，通过常识推理保持长序列身份一致性。

Abstract: Generalizing across unknown targets is critical for open-world perception, yet existing 3D Multi-Object Tracking (3D MOT) pipelines remain limited by closed-set assumptions and ``semantic-blind'' heuristics. To address this, we propose Next-step Open-Vocabulary Autoregression (NOVA), an innovative paradigm that shifts 3D tracking from traditional fragmented distance-based matching toward generative spatio-temporal semantic modeling. NOVA reformulates 3D trajectories as structured spatio-temporal semantic sequences, enabling the simultaneous encoding of physical motion continuity and deep linguistic priors. By leveraging the autoregressive capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), we transform the tracking task into a principled process of next-step sequence completion. This mechanism allows the model to explicitly utilize the hierarchical structure of language space to resolve fine-grained semantic ambiguities and maintain identity consistency across complex long-range sequences through high-level commonsense reasoning. Extensive experiments on nuScenes, V2X-Seq-SPD, and KITTI demonstrate the superior performance of NOVA. Notably, on the nuScenes dataset, NOVA achieves an AMOTA of 22.41% for Novel categories, yielding a significant 20.21% absolute improvement over the baseline. These gains are realized through a compact 0.5B autoregressive model. Code will be available at https://github.com/xifen523/NOVA.

</details>


### [95] [Can we Trust Unreliable Voxels? Exploring 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06279)
*Wenxin Li,Kunyu Peng,Di Wen,Junwei Zheng,Jiale Wei,Mengfei Duan,Yuheng Zhang,Rui Fan,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了OccNL基准和DPR-Occ方法，用于解决3D语义占据预测中标签噪声问题，通过双源部分标签推理在极端噪声下保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的3D体素标注存在结构伪影和动态拖尾效应等固有噪声，这对自动驾驶系统的安全性构成威胁。现有2D标签噪声学习方法在稀疏3D体素空间中会灾难性失效，需要专门针对3D占据预测的噪声鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出DPR-Occ框架，通过双源部分标签推理构建可靠监督：结合时间模型记忆和表示层结构相似性，动态扩展和修剪候选标签集，保留真实语义同时抑制噪声传播。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI上的实验表明，DPR-Occ在极端噪声下防止了几何和语义崩溃。即使在90%标签噪声下，相比现有方法仍能获得显著性能提升（最高2.57% mIoU和13.91% IoU）。

Conclusion: OccNL基准和DPR-Occ方法为动态环境中安全关键的机器人感知提供了可靠基础，通过桥接标签噪声学习和3D感知，解决了现有范式中的关键脆弱性。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction is a cornerstone of robotic perception, yet real-world voxel annotations are inherently corrupted by structural artifacts and dynamic trailing effects. This raises a critical but underexplored question: can autonomous systems safely rely on such unreliable occupancy supervision? To systematically investigate this issue, we establish OccNL, the first benchmark dedicated to 3D occupancy under occupancy-asymmetric and dynamic trailing noise. Our analysis reveals a fundamental domain gap: state-of-the-art 2D label noise learning strategies collapse catastrophically in sparse 3D voxel spaces, exposing a critical vulnerability in existing paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose DPR-Occ, a principled label noise-robust framework that constructs reliable supervision through dual-source partial label reasoning. By synergizing temporal model memory with representation-level structural affinity, DPR-Occ dynamically expands and prunes candidate label sets to preserve true semantics while suppressing noise propagation. Extensive experiments on SemanticKITTI demonstrate that DPR-Occ prevents geometric and semantic collapse under extreme corruption. Notably, even at 90% label noise, our method achieves significant performance gains (up to 2.57% mIoU and 13.91% IoU) over existing label noise learning baselines adapted to the 3D occupancy prediction task. By bridging label noise learning and 3D perception, OccNL and DPR-Occ provide a reliable foundation for safety-critical robotic perception in dynamic environments. The benchmark and source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/mylwx/OccNL.

</details>


### [96] [Spatial Calibration of Diffuse LiDARs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06531)
*Nikhil Behari,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种针对漫反射直接飞行时间LiDAR的空间标定方法，通过扫描反光贴片恢复每个像素在RGB图像平面中的有效区域和相对空间灵敏度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漫反射直接飞行时间LiDAR通过聚合大瞬时视场内的光子返回形成深度直方图，违反了标准LiDAR-RGB标定的单射线假设，需要新的空间标定方法

Method: 使用扫描的反光贴片结合背景减除，恢复每个像素的响应图，估计每个漫反射LiDAR像素在共置RGB图像平面中的足迹（有效支持区域）和相对空间灵敏度

Result: 在ams OSRAM TMF8828上演示了该方法，获得了明确的LiDAR到RGB对应关系，用于跨模态对齐和融合

Conclusion: 该方法为漫反射LiDAR提供了简单有效的空间标定程序，解决了传统标定方法不适用的问题，实现了准确的跨模态对齐

Abstract: Diffuse direct time-of-flight LiDARs report per-pixel depth histograms formed by aggregating photon returns over a wide instantaneous field of view, violating the single-ray assumption behind standard LiDAR-RGB calibration. We present a simple spatial calibration procedure that estimates, for each diffuse LiDAR pixel, its footprint (effective support region) and relative spatial sensitivity in a co-located RGB image plane. Using a scanned retroreflective patch with background subtraction, we recover per-pixel response maps that provide an explicit LiDAR-to-RGB correspondence for cross-modal alignment and fusion. We demonstrate the method on the ams OSRAM TMF8828.

</details>


### [97] [Lyapunov Probes for Hallucination Detection in Large Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06081)
*Bozhi Luan,Gen Li,Yalan Qin,Jifeng Guo,Yun Zhou,Faguo Wu,Hongwei Zheng,Wenjun Wu,Zhaoxin Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Lyapunov Probes方法，将LLMs/MLLMs视为动力系统，通过稳定性理论检测幻觉：事实知识对应稳定平衡点，幻觉发生在知识过渡区边界，通过轻量网络和导数约束实现稳定区域检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将幻觉检测视为简单分类任务，忽略了LLMs/MLLMs作为复杂系统的动力学特性。本文从动力系统稳定性理论角度重新审视幻觉问题，认为事实知识在表示空间中对应稳定平衡点，而幻觉发生在稳定与不稳定区域的过渡边界。

Method: 提出Lyapunov Probes：轻量网络，通过导数约束训练确保在输入扰动下置信度单调衰减。采用两阶段训练过程：1）系统扰动分析识别知识过渡区；2）训练探针区分稳定事实区域和不稳定幻觉易发区域。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型上的实验表明，该方法相比现有基线有显著改进，能可靠区分稳定事实区域和不稳定幻觉区域，验证了动力系统稳定性理论在幻觉检测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 将LLMs/MLLMs视为动力系统，从稳定性理论角度分析幻觉问题具有理论价值和实际效果。Lyapunov Probes方法为幻觉检测提供了新视角，通过捕捉知识过渡区的边界特性，实现了更可靠的幻觉检测。

Abstract: We address hallucination detection in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by framing the problem through the lens of dynamical systems stability theory. Rather than treating hallucination as a straightforward classification task, we conceptualize (M)LLMs as dynamical systems, where factual knowledge is represented by stable equilibrium points within the representation space. Our main insight is that hallucinations tend to arise at the boundaries of knowledge-transition regions separating stable and unstable zones. To capture this phenomenon, we propose Lyapunov Probes: lightweight networks trained with derivative-based stability constraints that enforce a monotonic decay in confidence under input perturbations. By performing systematic perturbation analysis and applying a two-stage training process, these probes reliably distinguish between stable factual regions and unstable, hallucination-prone regions. Experiments on diverse datasets and models demonstrate consistent improvements over existing baselines.

</details>


### [98] [DeepSight: Bridging Depth Maps and Language with a Depth-Driven Multimodal Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06090)
*Hao Yang,Hongbo Zhang,Yanyan Zhao,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeepSight是首个专门用于深度感知的多模态大语言模型，通过构建深度图像-文本对数据集和改进视觉编码器，显著提升了三维场景理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在深度信息理解方面存在不足，难以准确解析视觉数据中的三维空间信息，这限制了它们在需要空间推理任务中的应用。

Method: 1) 构建新颖的深度图像-文本对数据集和深度指令数据集，使用GLPN模型从视觉图像生成深度图，GPT-4生成对应深度指令；2) 改进CLIP中的ViT编码器以融入局部物体信息，更好地捕捉深度连续变化；3) 开发全面的深度问答基准进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，DeepSight显著增强了深度感知能力和下游任务性能，在多模态三维理解方面取得了实质性进展。

Conclusion: DeepSight作为首个专用深度MLLM，通过创新的数据集构建和模型改进，有效解决了现有MLLM在深度理解方面的局限性，为三维场景理解开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance across various tasks such as image captioning and visual question answer(VQA); however, they often struggle to accurately interpret depth information inherent in visual data. In this work, we introduce DeepSight, the first dedicated depth MLLM designed to enhance three-dimensional scene understanding. Unlike conventional methods that align RGB image encodings with text, our approach takes advantage of the unique characteristics of depth images: single-channel grayscale images where the pixel values directly reflect depth cues to improve spatial reasoning. To address challenges associated with limited depth data and the inadequacy of simple channel replication, we construct a novel depth image-text pair dataset and a depth instruction dataset. Depth maps are generated from visual images using the GLPN model, and GPT-4 is employed to curate corresponding depth instructions, an approach validated by LLaVA. Additionally, we modify the ViT encoder in CLIP to incorporate local object information, thereby capturing the subtle continuous variations of depth more effectively. To evaluate the performance of our model, we develop a comprehensive depth question answer benchmark based on existing depth image datasets, which rigorously assesses understanding in typical depth map scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that DeepSight significantly enhances depth perception and downstream task performance, marking a substantial step forward in multimodal three-dimensional understanding.

</details>


### [99] [FedARKS: Federated Aggregation via Robust and Discriminative Knowledge Selection and Integration for Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06122)
*Xin Xu,Binchang Ma,Zhixi Yu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedARKS框架解决FedDG-ReID中全局特征不足和平均聚合忽略客户端差异的问题，通过鲁棒知识提取和知识选择机制提升模型泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦域泛化行人重识别方法依赖全局特征表示和简单平均聚合，存在两个局限：1) 仅用全局特征难以捕捉细粒度域不变局部细节；2) 均匀参数平均忽略客户端在鲁棒特征提取能力上的差异，稀释高质量客户端贡献

Method: 提出FedARKS框架，包含两个机制：RK（鲁棒知识）机制提取细粒度局部特征，KS（知识选择）机制根据客户端特征提取能力进行差异化聚合

Result: 论文未提供具体实验结果，但从方法设计看应能提升模型在未见域上的泛化性能，同时保护客户端数据隐私

Conclusion: FedARKS通过鲁棒知识提取和差异化知识选择，解决了联邦域泛化行人重识别中的特征表示和聚合问题，有望提升模型泛化能力

Abstract: The application of federated domain generalization in person re-identification (FedDG-ReID) aims to enhance the model's generalization ability in unseen domains while protecting client data privacy. However, existing mainstream methods typically rely on global feature representations and simple averaging operations for model aggregation, leading to two limitations in domain generalization: (1) Using only global features makes it difficult to capture subtle, domain-invariant local details (such as accessories or textures); (2) Uniform parameter averaging treats all clients as equivalent, ignoring their differences in robust feature extraction capabilities, thereby diluting the contributions of high quality clients. To address these issues, we propose a novel federated learning framework, Federated Aggregation via Robust and Discriminative Knowledge Selection and Integration (FedARKS), comprising two mechanisms: RK (Robust Knowledge) and KS (Knowledge Selection).

</details>


### [100] [Cross-Resolution Distribution Matching for Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06136)
*Feiyang Chen,Hongpeng Pan,Haonan Xu,Xinyu Duan,Yang Yang,Zhefeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RMD提出了一种跨分辨率分布匹配蒸馏框架，通过logSNR曲线划分时间步、分布匹配和噪声重注入机制，实现高保真度的少步多分辨率级联推理，显著加速扩散模型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散蒸馏方法受限于去噪过程，时间步减少已基本饱和；部分时间步低分辨率生成虽然能加速，但存在跨分辨率分布差距导致质量下降的问题。

Method: 1. 使用logSNR曲线为每个分辨率划分时间步区间；2. 引入logSNR映射补偿分辨率引起的偏移；3. 沿分辨率轨迹进行分布匹配，缩小低分辨率生成器分布与教师高分辨率分布的差距；4. 在上采样过程中加入预测噪声重注入机制稳定训练。

Result: RMD在保持高保真生成的同时显著加速推理：SDXL上达到33.4倍加速，Wan2.1-14B上达到25.6倍加速，同时保持高视觉保真度。

Conclusion: RMD通过跨分辨率分布匹配蒸馏有效解决了现有方法在加速扩散模型生成时的局限性，实现了高质量、高效率的多分辨率级联推理。

Abstract: Diffusion distillation is central to accelerating image and video generation, yet existing methods are fundamentally limited by the denoising process, where step reduction has largely saturated. Partial timestep low-resolution generation can further accelerate inference, but it suffers noticeable quality degradation due to cross-resolution distribution gaps. We propose Cross-Resolution Distribution Matching Distillation (RMD), a novel distillation framework that bridges cross-resolution distribution gaps for high-fidelity, few-step multi-resolution cascaded inference. Specifically, RMD divides the timestep intervals for each resolution using logarithmic signal-to-noise ratio (logSNR) curves, and introduces logSNR-based mapping to compensate for resolution-induced shifts. Distribution matching is conducted along resolution trajectories to reduce the gap between low-resolution generator distributions and the teacher's high-resolution distribution. In addition, a predicted-noise re-injection mechanism is incorporated during upsampling to stabilize training and improve synthesis quality. Quantitative and qualitative results show that RMD preserves high-fidelity generation while accelerating inference across various backbones. Notably, RMD achieves up to 33.4X speedup on SDXL and 25.6X on Wan2.1-14B, while preserving high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [101] [Spatial Colour Mixing Illusions as a Perception Stress Test for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06141)
*Nicoleta-Nina Basoc,Adrian Cosma,Emilian Radoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现视觉语言模型(VLMs)对结构化颜色扭曲敏感，即使人类能轻松识别，模型也会产生错误预测。通过空间颜色混合扭曲测试，发现模型性能随扭曲程度增加而急剧下降，且扩大语言模型规模无法缓解此问题。人类表现显著优于VLMs，简单的人类启发式预处理能部分恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在基准测试中表现良好，但存在系统性感知弱点：即使底层场景对人类来说易于识别，像素值的结构化大幅变化也会导致模型产生自信但荒谬的预测。本研究旨在探索这种人类与模型之间的感知差距。

Method: 使用空间颜色混合方法，这是一种程序化的颜色扭曲家族，在自然图像上叠加结构化模式（包括RGB和Ostwald颜色系统）。提出了八种空间颜色混合变体的框架，在四个数据集上评估了来自三个模型家族的九个VLMs。同时进行了包含61名参与者的人类研究，比较人类与模型在相同扭曲下的表现。

Result: 在所有模型和数据集中，随着扭曲程度增加，准确率急剧下降。扩大语言模型规模并不能可靠地缓解这种失败。人类研究显示，在动物识别数据集上，人类在相同扭曲下的表现显著优于VLMs。简单的受人类启发的预处理步骤能部分恢复几种扭曲类型的性能。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型对结构化颜色扭曲存在系统性脆弱性，这揭示了模型感知与人类感知之间的重要差距。研究结果支持采用感知感知的预处理和工具使用作为提高VLM鲁棒性的实用策略。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong benchmark results, yet can exhibit systematic perceptual weaknesses: structured, large changes to pixel values can cause confident yet nonsensical predictions, even when the underlying scene remains easily recognizable to humans. We study this gap using Spatial Colour Mixing, a programmatic family of colour distortions that overlays structured patterns (in both RGB and Ostwald colour systems) onto natural images. We introduce a framework of eight spatial colour mixing variants and evaluate nine VLMs across three model families on four datasets. Across models and datasets, accuracy degrades sharply with increasing distortion, and scaling the language model does not reliably mitigate the failure. In a human study with 61 participants on an animal recognition dataset, humans substantially outperform VLMs under the same distortions. Finally, we show that a simple human-inspired preprocessing step recovers a meaningful portion of performance for several distortion types, motivating perception-aware preprocessing and tool-use as practical strategies for improving VLM robustness.

</details>


### [102] [Longitudinal NSCLC Treatment Progression via Multimodal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06147)
*Massimiliano Mantegna,Elena Mulero Ayllón,Alice Natalina Caragliano,Francesco Di Feola,Claudia Tacconi,Michele Fiore,Edy Ippolito,Carlo Greco,Sara Ramella,Philippe C. Cattin,Paolo Soda,Matteo Tortora,Valerio Guarrasi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出虚拟治疗框架，将非小细胞肺癌放疗期间肿瘤演化建模为剂量感知的多模态条件图像翻译问题，通过生成对抗网络和扩散模型合成放疗后的CT随访图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放疗期间预测肿瘤演化是临床关键挑战，特别是当纵向变化由解剖结构和治疗共同驱动时，需要开发能够模拟治疗诱导解剖变化的工具。

Method: 提出虚拟治疗框架，将NSCLC进展建模为剂量感知多模态条件图像翻译问题。基于CT扫描、基线临床变量和辐射剂量增量，合成反映治疗诱导解剖变化的随访CT图像。使用222名III期NSCLC患者的895个CT扫描数据集，在2D和2.5D配置下对GAN和扩散模型进行基准测试。

Result: 定量和定性结果表明，扩散模型从多模态剂量感知条件中获益更一致，比GAN基线产生更稳定、解剖学上更合理的肿瘤演化轨迹。

Conclusion: 虚拟治疗框架作为NSCLC计算机模拟治疗监测和自适应放疗研究的工具有潜力，扩散模型在生成稳定、解剖学合理的肿瘤演化轨迹方面表现更优。

Abstract: Predicting tumor evolution during radiotherapy is a clinically critical challenge, particularly when longitudinal changes are driven by both anatomy and treatment. In this work, we introduce a Virtual Treatment (VT) framework that formulates non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) progression as a dose-aware multimodal conditional image-to-image translation problem. Given a CT scan, baseline clinical variables, and a specified radiation dose increment, VT aims to synthesize plausible follow-up CT images reflecting treatment-induced anatomical changes. We evaluate the proposed framework on a longitudinal dataset of 222 stage III NSCLC patients, comprising 895 CT scans acquired during radiotherapy under irregular clinical schedules. The generative process is conditioned on delivered dose increments together with demographic and tumor-related clinical variables. Representative GAN-based and diffusion-based models are benchmarked across 2D and 2.5D configurations. Quantitative and qualitative results indicate that diffusion-based models benefit more consistently from multimodal, dose-aware conditioning and produce more stable and anatomically plausible tumor evolution trajectories than GAN-based baselines, supporting the potential of VT as a tool for in-silico treatment monitoring and adaptive radiotherapy research in NSCLC.

</details>


### [103] [FreeOcc: Training-free Panoptic Occupancy Prediction via Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06166)
*Andrew Caunes,Thierry Chateau,Vincent Fremont*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeOcc：无需训练的3D语义和全景占据预测方法，利用预训练基础模型从多视角图像恢复语义和几何信息，在未见环境中实现部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于相机的3D占据预测方法通常需要昂贵的密集3D监督或需要在目标域数据上训练模型，限制了在未见环境中的部署。需要一种无需训练的方法来解决这一限制。

Method: 使用预训练基础模型：1) 可提示的基础分割模型提取每视角全景先验；2) 重建基础模型恢复度量3D点；3) 深度和置信度感知过滤将可靠标签提升到3D；4) 确定性细化堆栈进行体素化；5) 通过拟合和合并当前视角3D框候选恢复实例。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes上：无需训练达到16.9 mIoU和16.5 RayIoU，与最先进的弱监督方法相当；作为伪标签生成管道训练下游模型达到21.1 RayIoU，超越先前弱监督基线；在无需训练和弱监督全景占据预测中分别达到3.1 RayPQ和3.9 RayPQ。

Conclusion: FreeOcc展示了基础模型驱动的感知作为实现无需训练3D场景理解的实用途径，为部署在未见环境中提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Semantic and panoptic occupancy prediction for road scene analysis provides a dense 3D representation of the ego vehicle's surroundings. Current camera-only approaches typically rely on costly dense 3D supervision or require training models on data from the target domain, limiting deployment in unseen environments. We propose FreeOcc, a training-free pipeline that leverages pretrained foundation models to recover both semantics and geometry from multi-view images. FreeOcc extracts per-view panoptic priors with a promptable foundation segmentation model and prompt-to-taxonomy rules, and reconstructs metric 3D points with a reconstruction foundation model. Depth- and confidence- aware filtering lifts reliable labels into 3D, which are fused over time and voxelized with a deterministic refinement stack. For panoptic occupancy, instances are recovered by fitting and merging robust current-view 3D box candidates, enabling instance-aware occupancy without any learned 3D model. On Occ3D-nuScenes, FreeOcc achieves 16.9 mIoU and 16.5 RayIoU train-free, on par with state-of-the-art weakly supervised methods. When employed as a pseudo-label generation pipeline for training downstream models, it achieves 21.1 RayIoU, surpassing the previous state-of-the-art weakly supervised baseline. Furthermore, FreeOcc sets new baselines for both train-free and weakly supervised panoptic occupancy prediction, achieving 3.1 RayPQ and 3.9 RayPQ, respectively. These results highlight foundation-model-driven perception as a practical route to training-free 3D scene understanding.

</details>


### [104] [A Semi-Supervised Framework for Breast Ultrasound Segmentation with Training-Free Pseudo-Label Generation and Label Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06167)
*Ruili Li,Jiayi Ding,Ruiyu Li,Yilun Jin,Shiwen Ge,Yuwen Zeng,Xiaoyong Zhang,Eichi Takaya,Jan Vrba,Noriyasu Homma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于乳腺超声图像分割的半监督框架，通过免训练的伪标签生成和标签精炼，利用视觉语言模型生成结构一致的伪标签，在仅有2.5%标注数据下达到全监督模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督学习在乳腺超声图像分割中，由于标注数据极度有限，生成的伪标签不稳定，导致监督不准确和性能下降。现有视觉语言模型在医学图像上效果有限，因为领域特定的提示难以迁移。

Method: 1) 利用简单外观描述（如"暗色椭圆形"）实现自然图像到医学图像的跨域结构迁移，让视觉语言模型生成结构一致的伪标签；2) 用这些伪标签预热静态教师模型，捕获乳腺病变的全局结构先验；3) 结合指数移动平均教师，引入不确定性熵加权融合和自适应不确定性引导的反向对比学习来改善边界判别。

Result: 在四个乳腺超声数据集上的实验表明，该方法在仅有2.5%标注数据下就能达到与全监督模型相当的性能，显著优于现有半监督方法。

Conclusion: 该方法具有良好的可扩展性：对于其他成像模态或疾病，只需提供全局外观描述即可获得可靠的伪监督，能够在有限标注下实现可扩展的半监督医学图像分割。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for breast ultrasound (BUS) image segmentation, but it often suffers from unstable pseudo labels under extremely limited annotations, leading to inaccurate supervision and degraded performance. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) provide a new opportunity for pseudo-label generation, yet their effectiveness on BUS images remains limited because domain-specific prompts are difficult to transfer.
  To address this issue, we propose a semi-supervised framework with training-free pseudo-label generation and label refinement. By leveraging simple appearance-based descriptions (e.g., dark oval), our method enables cross-domain structural transfer between natural and medical images, allowing VLMs to generate structurally consistent pseudo labels. These pseudo labels are used to warm up a static teacher that captures global structural priors of breast lesions. Combined with an exponential moving average teacher, we further introduce uncertainty entropy weighted fusion and adaptive uncertainty-guided reverse contrastive learning to improve boundary discrimination.
  Experiments on four BUS datasets demonstrate that our method achieves performance comparable to fully supervised models even with only 2.5% labeled data, significantly outperforming existing SSL approaches. Moreover, the proposed paradigm is readily extensible: for other imaging modalities or diseases, only a global appearance description is required to obtain reliable pseudo supervision, enabling scalable semi-supervised medical image segmentation under limited annotations.

</details>


### [105] [JOPP-3D: Joint Open Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation on Point Clouds and Panoramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06168)
*Sandeep Inuganti,Hideaki Kanayama,Kanta Shimizu,Mahdi Chamseddine,Soichiro Yokota,Didier Stricker,Jason Rambach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JOPP-3D是一个开放词汇语义分割框架，通过联合利用全景图像和点云数据，实现语言驱动的场景理解，在2D和3D开放与封闭词汇语义分割上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决3D点云和全景图像跨模态语义分割的挑战，包括标注数据稀缺和固定标签模型适应性有限的问题，实现更灵活的语言驱动场景理解。

Method: 将RGB-D全景图像转换为切向透视图像和3D点云，利用这两种模态提取和对齐基础视觉语言特征，通过自然语言查询生成两种输入模态的语义掩码。

Result: 在Stanford-2D-3D-s和ToF-360数据集上的实验表明，JOPP-3D能够生成跨全景和3D域的一致且有语义意义的分割结果，在开放和封闭词汇2D和3D语义分割上相比SOTA有显著提升。

Conclusion: JOPP-3D框架成功实现了跨模态的开放词汇语义分割，通过联合利用全景和点云数据，为语言驱动的场景理解提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation across visual modalities such as 3D point clouds and panoramic images remains a challenging task, primarily due to the scarcity of annotated data and the limited adaptability of fixed-label models. In this paper, we present JOPP-3D, an open-vocabulary semantic segmentation framework that jointly leverages panoramic and point cloud data to enable language-driven scene understanding. We convert RGB-D panoramic images into their corresponding tangential perspective images and 3D point clouds, then use these modalities to extract and align foundational vision-language features. This allows natural language querying to generate semantic masks on both input modalities. Experimental evaluation on the Stanford-2D-3D-s and ToF-360 datasets demonstrates the capability of JOPP-3D to produce coherent and semantically meaningful segmentations across panoramic and 3D domains. Our proposed method achieves a significant improvement compared to the SOTA in open and closed vocabulary 2D and 3D semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [106] [Optimizing 3D Diffusion Models for Medical Imaging via Multi-Scale Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06173)
*Yueying Tian,Xudong Han,Meng Zhou,Rodrigo Aviles-Espinosa,Rupert Young,Philip Birch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用强化学习（RL）和多尺度反馈增强3D扩散模型的方法，通过结合2D切片评估和3D体积分析来优化局部纹理细节和全局结构一致性，在医学图像生成任务中取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型已成为3D医学图像生成的有力工具，但标准训练目标与临床相关性之间存在差距。需要开发能够同时优化局部细节和全局结构的方法，以生成更高质量的医学图像用于下游临床任务。

Method: 首先在MRI体积数据上预训练3D扩散模型建立生成先验，然后使用近端策略优化（PPO）进行微调，通过结合2D切片评估和3D体积分析的新颖奖励系统提供多尺度反馈。

Result: 在BraTS 2019和OASIS-1数据集上的实验表明，RL反馈有效引导生成过程向更高质量分布发展。定量分析显示Fréchet Inception Distance（FID）显著改善，合成数据在肿瘤和疾病分类任务中比未优化基线表现更好。

Conclusion: 提出的强化学习多尺度反馈方法能够有效增强3D扩散模型的生成质量，生成的医学图像在临床相关下游任务中表现出更好的实用性，为医学图像生成提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for 3D medical image generation, yet bridging the gap between standard training objectives and clinical relevance remains a challenge. This paper presents a method to enhance 3D diffusion models using Reinforcement Learning (RL) with multi-scale feedback. We first pretrain a 3D diffusion model on MRI volumes to establish a robust generative prior. Subsequently, we fine-tune the model using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), guided by a novel reward system that integrates both 2D slice-wise assessments and 3D volumetric analysis. This combination allows the model to simultaneously optimize for local texture details and global structural coherence. We validate our framework on the BraTS 2019 and OASIS-1 datasets. Our results indicate that incorporating RL feedback effectively steers the generation process toward higher quality distributions. Quantitative analysis reveals significant improvements in Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and, crucially, the synthetic data demonstrates enhanced utility in downstream tumor and disease classification tasks compared to non-optimized baselines.

</details>


### [107] [Making Training-Free Diffusion Segmentors Scale with the Generative Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06178)
*Benyuan Meng,Qianqian Xu,Zitai Wang,Xiaochun Cao,Longtao Huang,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两种技术（自动聚合和逐像素重缩放）来解决训练免费扩散分割器中注意力图与全局表示之间的差距，提升生成模型在分割任务中的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练免费扩散分割方法未能充分利用更强大生成模型的潜力，存在注意力图与全局表示之间的差距，以及文本token分数不平衡导致语义相关性不准确的问题

Method: 提出两种技术：1) 自动聚合：将多个头和层的交叉注意力图整合为统一的全局表示；2) 逐像素重缩放：解决不同文本token之间的分数不平衡问题，提高语义相关性准确性

Result: 在标准语义分割基准测试中表现出改进性能，并成功集成到生成技术中，展示了广泛的适用性

Conclusion: 通过自动聚合和逐像素重缩放技术，训练免费扩散分割方法能够更好地利用生成能力，解决现有方法的可扩展性问题

Abstract: As powerful generative models, text-to-image diffusion models have recently been explored for discriminative tasks. A line of research focuses on adapting a pre-trained diffusion model to semantic segmentation without any further training, leading to what training-free diffusion segmentors. These methods typically rely on cross-attention maps from the model's attention layers, which are assumed to capture semantic relationships between image pixels and text tokens. Ideally, such approaches should benefit from more powerful diffusion models, i.e., stronger generative capability should lead to better segmentation. However, we observe that existing methods often fail to scale accordingly. To understand this issue, we identify two underlying gaps: (i) cross-attention is computed across multiple heads and layers, but there exists a discrepancy between these individual attention maps and a unified global representation. (ii) Even when a global map is available, it does not directly translate to accurate semantic correlation for segmentation, due to score imbalances among different text tokens. To bridge these gaps, we propose two techniques: auto aggregation and per-pixel rescaling, which together enable training-free segmentation to better leverage generative capability. We evaluate our approach on standard semantic segmentation benchmarks and further integrate it into a generative technique, demonstrating both improved performance broad applicability. Codes are at https://github.com/Darkbblue/goca.

</details>


### [108] [Towards Motion Turing Test: Evaluating Human-Likeness in Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06181)
*Mingzhe Li,Mengyin Liu,Zekai Wu,Xincheng Lin,Junsheng Zhang,Ming Yan,Zengye Xie,Changwang Zhang,Chenglu Wen,Lan Xu,Siqi Shen,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Motion Turing Test框架和HHMotion数据集，用于评估人形机器人动作的人类相似度，发现当前机器人动作仍与人类有明显差距，并建立了自动评估模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受图灵测试启发，希望建立客观评估人形机器人动作人类相似度的框架。当前机器人动作看起来越来越自然，但缺乏系统性的评估方法来判断其与人类动作的真实相似度。

Method: 1) 提出Motion Turing Test框架，让人类观察者仅基于运动学信息区分机器人/人类动作；2) 创建HHMotion数据集，包含15个动作类别的1000个运动序列，使用SMPL-X统一表示；3) 招募30名标注者对每个姿势的人类相似度进行0-5评分；4) 建立自动预测人类相似度分数的基线模型。

Result: 1) 分析显示人形机器人动作仍与人类有明显偏差，特别是在跳跃、拳击、跑步等动态动作中；2) 发现当前多模态大语言模型在评估动作人类相似度方面表现不足；3) 提出的简单基线模型优于多个基于LLM的当代方法。

Conclusion: 建立了系统评估人形机器人动作人类相似度的框架和数据集，揭示了当前机器人动作的局限性，并为未来研究提供了基准。数据集、代码和基准将公开以支持社区研究。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have achieved significant progress in motion generation and control, exhibiting movements that appear increasingly natural and human-like. Inspired by the Turing Test, we propose the Motion Turing Test, a framework that evaluates whether human observers can discriminate between humanoid robot and human poses using only kinematic information. To facilitate this evaluation, we present the Human-Humanoid Motion (HHMotion) dataset, which consists of 1,000 motion sequences spanning 15 action categories, performed by 11 humanoid models and 10 human subjects. All motion sequences are converted into SMPL-X representations to eliminate the influence of visual appearance. We recruited 30 annotators to rate the human-likeness of each pose on a 0-5 scale, resulting in over 500 hours of annotation. Analysis of the collected data reveals that humanoid motions still exhibit noticeable deviations from human movements, particularly in dynamic actions such as jumping, boxing, and running. Building on HHMotion, we formulate a human-likeness evaluation task that aims to automatically predict human-likeness scores from motion data. Despite recent progress in multimodal large language models, we find that they remain inadequate for assessing motion human-likeness. To address this, we propose a simple baseline model and demonstrate that it outperforms several contemporary LLM-based methods. The dataset, code, and benchmark will be publicly released to support future research in the community.

</details>


### [109] [SpaCRD: Multimodal Deep Fusion of Histology and Spatial Transcriptomics for Cancer Region Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06186)
*Shuailin Xue,Jun Wan,Lihua Zhang,Wenwen Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaCRD是一种基于迁移学习的方法，通过深度整合组织学图像和空间转录组数据，实现跨样本、跨平台/批次的可靠癌症区域检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于组织学图像的癌症区域检测方法容易因不同组织区域间的形态相似性而产生高假阳性率。空间转录组技术提供了详细的细胞表型和空间定位信息，为更准确的癌症区域检测提供了新机会，但现有方法无法有效整合这两种数据，特别是在跨样本和跨平台/批次的设置下。

Method: 提出SpaCRD方法，核心是类别正则化变分重建引导的双向交叉注意力融合网络，使模型能够从多个角度自适应地捕捉组织学特征和基因表达之间的潜在共表达模式。该方法基于迁移学习，一旦在源数据上训练，即可泛化到不同平台和批次的样本。

Result: 在涵盖多种疾病类型、平台和批次的23个匹配组织学-ST数据集上进行广泛基准分析，结果显示SpaCRD在癌症区域检测方面持续优于现有的8种最先进方法。

Conclusion: SpaCRD通过深度整合组织学图像和空间转录组数据，实现了跨样本、跨平台/批次的可靠癌症区域检测，为肿瘤微环境分析和治疗反应评估提供了更准确的工具。

Abstract: Accurate detection of cancer tissue regions (CTR) enables deeper analysis of the tumor microenvironment and offers crucial insights into treatment response. Traditional CTR detection methods, which typically rely on the rich cellular morphology in histology images, are susceptible to a high rate of false positives due to morphological similarities across different tissue regions. The groundbreaking advances in spatial transcriptomics (ST) provide detailed cellular phenotypes and spatial localization information, offering new opportunities for more accurate cancer region detection. However, current methods are unable to effectively integrate histology images with ST data, especially in the context of cross-sample and cross-platform/batch settings for accomplishing the CTR detection. To address this challenge, we propose SpaCRD, a transfer learning-based method that deeply integrates histology images and ST data to enable reliable CTR detection across diverse samples, platforms, and batches. Once trained on source data, SpaCRD can be readily generalized to accurately detect cancerous regions across samples from different platforms and batches. The core of SpaCRD is a category-regularized variational reconstruction-guided bidirectional cross-attention fusion network, which enables the model to adaptively capture latent co-expression patterns between histological features and gene expression from multiple perspectives. Extensive benchmark analysis on 23 matched histology-ST datasets spanning various disease types, platforms, and batches demonstrates that SpaCRD consistently outperforms existing eight state-of-the-art methods in CTR detection.

</details>


### [110] [Adaptive Language-Aware Image Reflection Removal Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06200)
*Siyan Fang,Yuntao Wang,Jinpu Zhang,Ziwen Li,Yuehuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALANet通过自适应语言感知网络处理不准确的语言描述，结合过滤和优化策略来提升复杂反射去除效果，并创建了CRLAV数据集进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像反射去除方法难以处理复杂反射，而反射图像中的模糊和扭曲干扰导致机器生成的语言描述不准确，这会损害语言引导的反射去除性能。

Method: 提出自适应语言感知网络(ALANet)，整合过滤策略减少语言负面影响并保留其优势，优化策略增强语言与视觉特征的对齐，利用语言线索从特征图中解耦特定层内容。

Result: ALANet在复杂反射去除方面超越了现有最先进方法，特别是在处理不准确语言输入时表现优异。

Conclusion: ALANet能够有效处理不准确的语言描述，通过自适应策略提升复杂反射去除性能，为解决语言引导反射去除中的语言不准确问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Existing image reflection removal methods struggle to handle complex reflections. Accurate language descriptions can help the model understand the image content to remove complex reflections. However, due to blurred and distorted interferences in reflected images, machine-generated language descriptions of the image content are often inaccurate, which harms the performance of language-guided reflection removal. To address this, we propose the Adaptive Language-Aware Network (ALANet) to remove reflections even with inaccurate language inputs. Specifically, ALANet integrates both filtering and optimization strategies. The filtering strategy reduces the negative effects of language while preserving its benefits, whereas the optimization strategy enhances the alignment between language and visual features. ALANet also utilizes language cues to decouple specific layer content from feature maps, improving its ability to handle complex reflections. To evaluate the model's performance under complex reflections and varying levels of language accuracy, we introduce the Complex Reflection and Language Accuracy Variance (CRLAV) dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that ALANet surpasses state-of-the-art methods for image reflection removal. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/fashyon/ALANet.

</details>


### [111] [Point-Supervised Skeleton-Based Human Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06201)
*Hongsong Wang,Yiqin Shen,Pengbo Yan,Jie Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于骨架时序动作分割的点监督框架，仅需每个动作片段标注一帧，通过多模态骨架数据、原型相似性方法和伪标签集成，显著降低标注成本并达到有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全监督方法需要昂贵的逐帧标注且对模糊动作边界敏感，而点监督方法可以大幅降低标注成本同时保持性能。

Method: 使用预训练统一模型编码多模态骨架数据（关节、骨骼、运动信息），提出原型相似性方法并结合能量函数和约束K-Medoids聚类生成伪标签，通过多模态伪标签集成指导模型训练。

Result: 在PKU-MMD（X-Sub和X-View）、MCFS-22和MCFS-130数据集上建立新基准，实验表明该方法达到有竞争力的性能，甚至超越一些全监督方法，同时显著减少标注工作量。

Conclusion: 提出的点监督框架为骨架动作分割提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案，在降低标注成本的同时保持了良好的性能，为实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Skeleton-based temporal action segmentation is a fundamental yet challenging task, playing a crucial role in enabling intelligent systems to perceive and respond to human activities. While fully-supervised methods achieve satisfactory performance, they require costly frame-level annotations and are sensitive to ambiguous action boundaries. To address these issues, we introduce a point-supervised framework for skeleton-based action segmentation, where only a single frame per action segment is labeled. We leverage multimodal skeleton data, including joint, bone, and motion information, encoded via a pretrained unified model to extract rich feature representations. To generate reliable pseudo-labels, we propose a novel prototype similarity method and integrate it with two existing methods: energy function and constrained K-Medoids clustering. Multimodal pseudo-label integration is proposed to enhance the reliability of the pseudo-label and guide the model training. We establish new benchmarks on PKU-MMD (X-Sub and X-View), MCFS-22, and MCFS-130, and implement baselines for point-supervised skeleton-based human action segmentation. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves competitive performance, even surpassing some fully-supervised methods while significantly reducing annotation effort.

</details>


### [112] [EntON: Eigenentropy-Optimized Neighborhood Densification in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06216)
*Miriam Jäger,Boris Jutzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EntON提出了一种基于特征熵优化的邻域密集化策略，用于3D高斯泼溅，通过交替使用梯度驱动和几何感知的密集化来平衡几何精度与渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS生成的高斯中心与物体几何对齐不佳，而专注于表面的重建方法常牺牲光度精度。需要一种能同时保持几何准确性和渲染质量的方法。

Method: 提出特征熵优化的邻域密集化策略EntON：计算每个高斯中心k近邻协方差矩阵的特征值得到特征熵，量化局部结构有序度。在交替优化框架中，结合标准梯度驱动密集化和特征熵感知密集化，优先在低特征熵（有序、平坦）区域密集化以捕捉几何细节，在高特征熵（无序、球形）区域修剪。

Result: 在DTU和TUM2TWIN数据集上的实验表明：几何精度提升达33%，渲染质量提升达7%，高斯数量减少达50%，训练时间减少达23%。

Conclusion: EntON通过避免不必要的场景扩展，在几何精度、渲染质量和效率之间实现了有利的平衡。

Abstract: We present a novel Eigenentropy-optimized neighboorhood densification strategy EntON in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for geometrically accurate and high-quality rendered 3D reconstruction. While standard 3DGS produces Gaussians whose centers and surfaces are poorly aligned with the underlying object geometry, surface-focused reconstruction methods frequently sacrifice photometric accuracy. In contrast to the conventional densification strategy, which relies on the magnitude of the view-space position gradient, our approach introduces a geometry-aware strategy to guide adaptive splitting and pruning. Specifically, we compute the 3D shape feature Eigenentropy from the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix in the k-nearest neighborhood of each Gaussian center, which quantifies the local structural order. These Eigenentropy values are integrated into an alternating optimization framework: During the optimization process, the algorithm alternates between (i) standard gradient-based densification, which refines regions via view-space gradients, and (ii) Eigenentropy-aware densification, which preferentially densifies Gaussians in low-Eigenentropy (ordered, flat) neighborhoods to better capture fine geometric details on the object surface, and prunes those in high-Eigenentropy (disordered, spherical) regions. We provide quantitative and qualitative evaluations on two benchmark datasets: small-scale DTU dataset and large-scale TUM2TWIN dataset, covering man-made objects and urban scenes. Experiments demonstrate that our Eigenentropy-aware alternating densification strategy improves geometric accuracy by up to 33% and rendering quality by up to 7%, while reducing the number of Gaussians by up to 50% and training time by up to 23%. Overall, EnTON achieves a favorable balance between geometric accuracy, rendering quality and efficiency by avoiding unnecessary scene expansion.

</details>


### [113] [Word-Anchored Temporal Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06220)
*Tianyi Wang,Xi Shao,Harry Cheng,Yinglong Wang,Mohan Kankanhalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAFL提出基于词级二元分类的时间伪造定位新范式，通过词锚定对齐、特征重映射和不对称损失函数，显著提升定位性能并降低计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间伪造定位方法存在特征粒度不对齐和计算成本高的问题，需要更高效准确的解决方案

Method: 1) 将任务从时间回归转为词级二元分类；2) 引入法证特征重对齐模块将预训练特征映射到法证空间；3) 设计基于伪影的不对称损失函数处理类别不平衡

Result: WAFL在数据集内和跨数据集设置下均显著优于现有方法，同时需要更少的可学习参数和更高的计算效率

Conclusion: 词锚定的时间伪造定位范式通过离散化分类、特征重对齐和不对称损失，有效解决了现有方法的局限性，实现了高性能和高效能

Abstract: Current temporal forgery localization (TFL) approaches typically rely on temporal boundary regression or continuous frame-level anomaly detection paradigms to derive candidate forgery proposals. However, they suffer not only from feature granularity misalignment but also from costly computation. To address these issues, we propose word-anchored temporal forgery localization (WAFL), a novel paradigm that shifts the TFL task from temporal regression and continuous localization to discrete word-level binary classification. Specifically, we first analyze the essence of temporal forgeries and identify the minimum meaningful forgery units, word tokens, and then align data preprocessing with the natural linguistic boundaries of speech. To adapt powerful pre-trained foundation backbones for feature extraction, we introduce the forensic feature realignment (FFR) module, mapping representations from the pre-trained semantic space to a discriminative forensic manifold. This allows subsequent lightweight linear classifiers to efficiently perform binary classification and accomplish the TFL task. Furthermore, to overcome the extreme class imbalance inherent to forgery detection, we design the artifact-centric asymmetric (ACA) loss, which breaks the standard precision-recall trade-off by dynamically suppressing overwhelming authentic gradients while asymmetrically prioritizing subtle forensic artifacts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WAFL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in localization performance under both in- and cross-dataset settings, while requiring substantially fewer learnable parameters and operating at high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [114] [Low-latency Event-based Object Detection with Spatially-Sparse Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06228)
*Haiqing Hao,Zhipeng Sui,Rong Zou,Zijia Dai,Nikola Zubić,Davide Scaramuzza,Wenhui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SSLA-Det：一种基于空间稀疏线性注意力的异步事件相机目标检测方法，通过状态级稀疏化实现高效并行训练和低延迟推理，在保持高精度的同时大幅减少计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
