We formalize this challenge as lifelong embodied navigation learning (LENL), where an agent is required to adapt to a sequence of navigation tasks spanning multiple scenes and diverse user instruction styles, while retaining previously learned knowledge. To tackle this problem, we propose Uni-Walker, a lifelong embodied navigation framework that decouples navigation knowledge into task-shared and task-specific components with Decoder Extension LoRA (DE-LoRA). To learn the shared knowledge, we design a knowledge inheritance strategy and an experts co-activation strategy to facilitate shared knowledge transfer and refinement across multiple navigation tasks. To learn the specific knowledge, we propose an expert subspace orthogonality constraint together and a navigation-specific chain-of-thought reasoning mechanism to capture specific knowledge and enhance instruction-style understanding. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of Uni-Walker for building universal navigation agents with lifelong learning.

</details>


### [191] [Multimodal Behavior Tree Generation: A Small Vision-Language Model for Robot Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06084)
*Cristiano Battistini,Riccardo Andrea Izzo,Gianluca Bardaro,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用开源多模态模型为机器人任务规划生成行为树，通过构建视觉-指令-行为树数据集，微调视觉语言模型，在家庭任务中达到87%成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型和小型语言模型已广泛用于机器人任务规划，视觉语言模型在图像理解方面表现优异。本研究旨在结合两者优势，使用紧凑的开源多模态模型生成可执行的行为树，解决现有数据集中缺乏视觉观察、指令与行为树关联的问题。

Method: 1. 从现有机器人数据集（Open X-Embodiment）构建视觉-指令-行为树关联数据集；2. 使用大模型作为教师，通过多阶段生成管道创建数据；3. 使用参数高效微调（PEFT）方法微调500M到4B参数的视觉语言模型；4. 生成与BehaviorTree.CPP库兼容的行为树。

Result: 1. 离线评估显示在结构和词汇指标上表现良好；2. 在线评估中，在先进的具身模拟器中执行家庭任务，微调的4B参数VLM达到87%的成功率；3. 性能接近最先进的闭源模型，但仅需少量计算资源。

Conclusion: 通过构建专门的数据集和参数高效微调，紧凑的开源多模态视觉语言模型能够有效生成机器人任务规划的行为树，在保持高性能的同时显著降低计算需求，为机器人任务规划提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large and small language models have been widely used for robotic task planning. At the same time, vision-language models (VLMs) have successfully tackled problems such as image captioning, scene understanding, and visual question answering. In this work, we combine these two approaches by deploying a compact, open-source multimodal model to generate behavior trees for robotic task planning. The main obstacle to achieving this goal is the lack of an existing dataset that links visual observations and instructions to executable behavior trees. We propose a method to construct such a dataset starting from existing robotic episodes (i.e., Open X-Embodiment), in which a large model serves as a teacher in a multi-stage generation pipeline. We use this dataset to fine-tune VLMs ranging from 500M to 4B parameters via parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). The generated behavior trees, compatible with the BehaviorTree.CPP library, are evaluated both offline, using structural and lexical metrics, and online through the execution of household tasks in a state-of-the-art embodied simulator. Our results demonstrate that our fine-tuned 4B-parameter VLM approaches the performance of state-of-the-art closed-source models, achieving an 87\% success rate while requiring only a fraction of the computational resources.

</details>


### [192] [Sticky-Glance: Robust Intent Recognition for Human Robot Collaboration via Single-Glance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06121)
*Yuzhi Lai,Shenghai Yuan,Peizheng Li,Andreas Zell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于物体中心的凝视定位框架，通过粘性凝视算法稳定意图识别，在动态多物体环境中实现高精度凝视交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝视是运动能力受限人群的重要交流方式，但在多物体环境中，由于凝视噪声、微眼跳、视角变化和动态物体等因素，实现鲁棒的基于凝视的意图识别具有挑战性

Method: 提出物体中心凝视定位框架，采用粘性凝视算法联合建模几何距离和方向趋势，稳定意图识别；引入连续共享控制和多模态交互范式

Result: 仅需3个凝视样本即可将意图锚定到物体上，动态目标跟踪率0.94，静态目标选择准确率0.98；任务持续时间减少近10%，相比基线方法在鲁棒性、效率和减少工作负荷方面均有提升

Conclusion: 该框架在多物体动态环境中实现了鲁棒高效的凝视交互，为运动能力受限人群提供了有效的交互解决方案

Abstract: Gaze is a valuable means of communication for impaired people with extremely limited motor capabilities. However, robust gaze-based intent recognition in multi-object environments is challenging due to gaze noise, micro-saccades, viewpoint changes, and dynamic objects. To address this, we propose an object-centric gaze grounding framework that stabilizes intent through a sticky-glance algorithm, jointly modeling geometric distance and direction trends. The inferred intent remains anchored to the object even under short glances with minimal 3 gaze samples, achieving a tracking rate of 0.94 for dynamic targets and selection accuracy of 0.98 for static targets. We further introduce a continuous shared control and multi-modal interaction paradigm, enabling high-readiness control and human-in-loop feedback, thereby reducing task duration for nearly 10 \%. Experiments across dynamic tracking, multi-perspective alignment, a baseline comparison, user studies, and ablation studies demonstrate improved robustness, efficiency, and reduced workload compared to representative baselines.

</details>


### [193] [A Hazard-Informed Data Pipeline for Robotics Physical Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06130)
*Alexei Odinokov,Rostislav Yavorskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于资产声明、漏洞枚举和危险驱动合成数据生成的机器人物理安全框架，连接传统风险工程与现代机器学习流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要将传统安全工程与数字孪生、合成数据生成和机器学习训练相结合，为机器人系统建立基于形式化危险本体论的安全边界学习。

Method: 基于显式资产声明、系统化漏洞枚举和危险驱动的合成数据生成的结构化框架，连接经典风险工程与现代机器学习流程。

Result: 建立了一个能够将经典安全工程、数字孪生仿真、合成数据生成和机器学习模型训练对齐的机器人物理安全框架。

Conclusion: 该框架成功连接了传统安全工程与现代机器学习方法，为机器人系统提供了基于形式化危险本体论的安全边界学习能力。

Abstract: This report presents a structured Robotics Physical Safety Framework based on explicit asset declaration, systematic vulnerability enumeration, and hazard-driven synthetic data generation. The approach bridges classical risk engineering with modern machine learning pipelines, enabling safety envelope learning grounded in a formalized hazard ontology. The key contribution of this framework is the alignment between classical safety engineering, digital twin simulation, synthetic data generation, and machine learning model training.

</details>


### [194] [Dual-Agent Multiple-Model Reinforcement Learning for Event-Triggered Human-Robot Co-Adaptation in Decoupled Task Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06163)
*Yaqi Li,Zhengqi Han,Huifang Liu,Steven W. Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于事件触发的双智能体多模型强化学习共享控制康复策略，用于6自由度上肢康复机器人，通过分解空间轴和自适应控制优化人机协作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定频率控制在复杂到达任务中常因逆运动学执行时间变化导致轨迹振荡，需要更安全高效的人机协同适应方法

Method: 1) 将复杂到达任务分解为解耦的空间轴；2) 患者通过二元命令控制主要到达方向，机器人自主管理正交校正运动；3) 提出事件驱动进展策略，仅当末端执行器进入目标路点的准入球时才触发控制动作；4) 引入双智能体多模型强化学习框架，人类智能体选择准入球半径反映速度-精度权衡，机器人智能体动态调整3D笛卡尔步长以补充用户认知状态

Result: 在仿真中训练并在混合环境中部署，该事件触发的DAMMRL方法有效抑制了路点抖动，平衡了空间精度与时间效率，显著提高了物体获取任务的成功率

Conclusion: 提出的共享控制康复策略通过事件驱动架构和自适应强化学习框架，实现了更安全高效的人机协同适应，为复杂上肢康复任务提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: This paper presents a shared-control rehabilitation policy for a custom 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) upper-limb robot that decomposes complex reaching tasks into decoupled spatial axes. The patient governs the primary reaching direction using binary commands, while the robot autonomously manages orthogonal corrective motions. Because traditional fixed-frequency control often induces trajectory oscillations due to variable inverse-kinematics execution times, an event-driven progression strategy is proposed. This architecture triggers subsequent control actions only when the end-effector enters an admission sphere centred on the immediate target waypoint, and was validated in a semi-virtual setup linking a physical pressure sensor to a MuJoCo simulation. To optimise human--robot co-adaptation safely and efficiently, this study introduces Dual Agent Multiple Model Reinforcement Learning (DAMMRL). This framework discretises decision characteristics: the human agent selects the admission sphere radius to reflect their inherent speed--accuracy trade-off, while the robot agent dynamically adjusts its 3D Cartesian step magnitudes to complement the user's cognitive state. Trained in simulation and deployed across mixed environments, this event-triggered DAMMRL approach effectively suppresses waypoint chatter, balances spatial precision with temporal efficiency, and significantly improves success rates in object acquisition tasks.

</details>


### [195] [DreamToNav: Generalizable Navigation for Robots via Generative Video Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06190)
*Valerii Serpiva,Jeffrin Sam,Chidera Simon,Hajira Amjad,Iana Zhura,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamToNav：利用生成视频模型实现直观人机交互控制的机器人导航框架，用户通过自然语言指令控制机器人，系统生成视频预测并提取可执行轨迹


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于固定路径点的导航方式不够直观，需要用户提供精确的坐标指令。DreamToNav旨在通过自然语言交互让机器人控制更加人性化，让用户能够用日常语言描述期望的机器人行为

Method: 1. 使用Qwen 2.5-VL-7B-Instruct将模糊的自然语言指令精炼为精确的视觉描述；2. 用这些描述条件化NVIDIA Cosmos 2.5视频生成模型，合成机器人执行任务的物理一致视频序列；3. 从合成视频中通过视觉姿态估计、机器人检测和轨迹恢复提取有效的运动学路径

Result: 在轮式移动机器人和四足机器人的室内导航任务中，DreamToNav实现了76.7%的成功率，最终目标误差通常在0.05-0.10米之间，轨迹跟踪误差低于0.15米

Conclusion: DreamToNav证明了从生成视频预测中提取的轨迹可以在不同运动平台的物理机器人上可靠执行，为避障和目标导向导航提供了统一的框架，无需特定任务工程

Abstract: We present DreamToNav, a novel autonomous robot framework that uses generative video models to enable intuitive, human-in-the-loop control. Instead of relying on rigid waypoint navigation, users provide natural language prompts (e.g. ``Follow the person carefully''), which the system translates into executable motion. Our pipeline first employs Qwen 2.5-VL-7B-Instruct to refine vague user instructions into precise visual descriptions. These descriptions condition NVIDIA Cosmos 2.5, a state-of-the-art video foundation model, to synthesize a physically consistent video sequence of the robot performing the task. From this synthetic video, we extract a valid kinematic path using visual pose estimation, robot detection and trajectory recovery. By treating video generation as a planning engine, DreamToNav allows robots to visually "dream" complex behaviors before executing them, providing a unified framework for obstacle avoidance and goal-directed navigation without task-specific engineering. We evaluate the approach on both a wheeled mobile robot and a quadruped robot in indoor navigation tasks. DreamToNav achieves a success rate of 76.7%, with final goal errors typically within 0.05-0.10 m and trajectory tracking errors below 0.15 m. These results demonstrate that trajectories extracted from generative video predictions can be reliably executed on physical robots across different locomotion platforms.

</details>


### [196] [KISS-IMU: Self-supervised Inertial Odometry with Motion-balanced Learning and Uncertainty-aware Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06205)
*Jiwon Choi,Hogyun Kim,Geonmo Yang,Juhui Lee,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KISS-IMU：一种无需地面真值监督的自监督惯性里程计框架，利用LiDAR ICP配准和位姿图优化作为监督信号，通过运动感知平衡训练和不确定性驱动自适应加权实现鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度神经网络的惯性里程计方法严重依赖地面真值数据进行训练，这限制了其在未见和多样化环境中的可扩展性和泛化能力。需要一种能够摆脱地面真值依赖的自监督方法。

Method: 提出KISS-IMU框架，使用简单的LiDAR-based ICP配准和位姿图优化作为监督信号，无需地面真值。采用两个关键原则：1）通过运动感知平衡训练保持IMU稳定；2）通过推理时的不确定性驱动自适应加权保持IMU强大。仅训练IMU网络，LiDAR仅作为轻量级监督信号。

Result: 在包括四足机器人在内的多种真实世界平台上进行了全面实验，评估了不同运动模式和场景下的性能。框架在无需联合多模态学习或地面真值监督的情况下确保了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: KISS-IMU成功消除了惯性里程计对地面真值的依赖，通过自监督学习实现了良好的泛化能力，为机器人系统在多样化环境中的惯性导航提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Inertial measurement units (IMUs), which provide high-frequency linear acceleration and angular velocity measurements, serve as fundamental sensing modalities in robotic systems. Recent advances in deep neural networks have led to remarkable progress in inertial odometry. However, the heavy reliance on ground truth data during training fundamentally limits scalability and generalization to unseen and diverse environments. We propose KISS-IMU, a novel self-supervised inertial odometry framework that eliminates ground truth dependency by leveraging simple LiDAR-based ICP registration and pose graph optimization as a supervisory signal. Our approach embodies two key principles: keeping the IMU stable through motion-aware balanced training and keeping the IMU strong through uncertainty-driven adaptive weighting during inference. To evaluate performance across diverse motion patterns and scenarios, we conducted comprehensive experiments on various real-world platforms, including quadruped robots. Importantly, we train only the IMU network in a self-supervised manner, with LiDAR serving solely as a lightweight supervisory signal rather than requiring additional learnable processes. This design enables the framework to ensure robustness without relying on joint multi-modal learning or ground truth supervision. The supplementary materials are available at https://sparolab.github.io/research/kiss_imu.

</details>


### [197] [Few-Shot Neural Differentiable Simulator: Real-to-Sim Rigid-Contact Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06218)
*Zhenhao Huang,Siyuan Luo,Bingyang Zhou,Ziqiu Zeng,Jason Pho,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种少样本真实到仿真的方法，结合解析模拟器的物理一致性和图神经网络的表示能力，通过少量真实数据校准模拟器生成大规模合成数据，实现完全可微分的物理仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析模拟器难以捕捉复杂接触动力学，而基于学习的模拟器通常需要大量昂贵的真实世界数据。需要一种方法既能保持物理一致性，又能用少量数据实现高精度仿真。

Method: 1) 使用少量真实数据校准解析模拟器生成大规模合成数据集；2) 引入基于网格的图神经网络隐式建模刚体前向动力学；3) 推导碰撞检测的替代梯度实现完全可微分。

Result: 该方法使基于学习的模拟器在复制真实世界轨迹方面优于可微分基线。可微分设计支持基于梯度的优化，在基于仿真的策略学习中验证了有效性，提高了仿真保真度和策略学习效率。

Conclusion: 少样本真实世界基础的可微分仿真为推进未来机器人操作和控制提供了强大方向，能够以最小监督提高仿真保真度，并提升策略学习效率。

Abstract: Accurate physics simulation is essential for robotic learning and control, yet analytical simulators often fail to capture complex contact dynamics, while learning-based simulators typically require large amounts of costly real-world data. To bridge this gap, we propose a few-shot real-to-sim approach that combines the physical consistency of analytical formulations with the representational capacity of graph neural network (GNN)-based models. Using only a small amount of real-world data, our method calibrates analytical simulators to generate large-scale synthetic datasets that capture diverse contact interactions. On this foundation, we introduce a mesh-based GNN that implicitly models rigid-body forward dynamics and derive surrogate gradients for collision detection, achieving full differentiability. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach enables learning-based simulators to outperform differentiable baselines in replicating real-world trajectories. In addition, the differentiable design supports gradient-based optimization, which we validate through simulation-based policy learning in multi-object interaction scenarios. Extensive experiments show that our framework not only improves simulation fidelity with minimal supervision but also increases the efficiency of policy learning. Taken together, these findings suggest that differentiable simulation with few-shot real-world grounding provides a powerful direction for advancing future robotic manipulation and control.

</details>


### [198] [Towards Robotic Lake Maintenance: Integrating SONAR and Satellite Data to Assist Human Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06266)
*Ahmed H. Elsayed,Christoph Manss,Tarek A. El-Mihoub,Andrej Lejman,Frederic Stahl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法，结合卫星遥感和无人水面船声纳技术，用于人工水体中沉水植被的检测与定位，以支持针对性杂草收割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工水体需要持续监测和维护以管理其生态系统，其中沉水植被会快速生长，需要收割以保持生态平衡。传统方法劳动强度大，需要更有效的监测和收割支持方法。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：第一阶段使用卫星遥感数据，通过水生植物和藻类指数检测沉水植被区域；第二阶段使用配备多波束声纳的无人水面船进行高分辨率水深测绘，精确定位和量化水生植被。

Result: 初步结果表明，整合卫星影像和水下声学传感来改善人工湖泊植被管理是可行的。该方法缩小了搜索范围，减少了人工操作员的工作量。

Conclusion: 这种两阶段方法实现了有效的人机协作，卫星数据指导无人水面船任务，船工利用详细的声纳地图进行针对性收割，使收割过程对操作员来说劳动强度更低。

Abstract: Artificial Water Bodies (AWBs) are human-made systems that require continuous monitoring due to their artificial biological processes. These systems demand regular maintenance to manage their ecosystems effectively. As a result of these artificial conditions, underwater vegetation can grow rapidly and must be harvested to preserve the ecological balance. This paper proposes a two-step approach to support targeted weed harvesting for the maintenance of artificial lakes. The first step is the initial detection of Submerged Aquatic Vegetation (SAV), also referred to in this paper as areas of interest, is performed using satellite-derived indices, specifically the Aquatic Plants and Algae (APA) index, which highlights submerged vegetation in water bodies. Subsequently, an Unmanned Surface Vehicle (USV) equipped with multibeam SOund NAvigation and Ranging (SONAR) performs high-resolution bathymetric mapping to locate and quantify aquatic vegetation precisely. This two-stage approach offers an effective human-robot collaboration, where satellite data guides the USV missions and boat skippers leverage detailed SONAR maps for targeted harvesting. This setup narrows the search space and reduces manual workload from human operators, making the harvesting process less labour-intensive for operators. Preliminary results demonstrate the feasibility of integrating satellite imagery and underwater acoustic sensing to improve vegetation management in artificial lakes.

</details>


### [199] [SuperSuit: An Isomorphic Bimodal Interface for Scalable Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06280)
*Tongqing Chen,Hang Wu,Jiasen Wang,Xiaotao Li,Zhu Jin,Lu Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperSuit是一个双模态数据采集框架，支持机器人遥操作和主动演示，通过共享运动学接口生成结构相同的关节空间轨迹，解决了移动机械臂长时程演示数据获取的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、长时程的演示数据对具身AI至关重要，但为轮式移动机械臂获取此类数据存在瓶颈。移动机械臂需要SE(2)运动与精确操作的连续协调，现有遥操作和可穿戴接口存在局限性。

Method: 提出SuperSuit双模态数据采集框架，支持机器人遥操作和主动演示两种模式，共享运动学接口。将自然人类步态映射到连续平面基座速度，使用严格同构的可穿戴臂，采用平移不变的delta关节表示进行策略训练。

Result: 主动模式演示吞吐量比遥操作基线高2.6倍；在固定数据集大小下，用主动演示替代遥操作数据可获得相当的政策性能；随着主动数据量增加，性能单调提升。

Conclusion: 跨采集模式的一致运动学表示能够实现长时程移动操作的可扩展数据采集，SuperSuit框架有效解决了移动机械臂演示数据获取的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: High-quality, long-horizon demonstrations are essential for embodied AI, yet acquiring such data for tightly coupled wheeled mobile manipulators remains a fundamental bottleneck. Unlike fixed-base systems, mobile manipulators require continuous coordination between $SE(2)$ locomotion and precise manipulation, exposing limitations in existing teleoperation and wearable interfaces. We present \textbf{SuperSuit}, a bimodal data acquisition framework that supports both robot-in-the-loop teleoperation and active demonstration under a shared kinematic interface. Both modalities produce structurally identical joint-space trajectories, enabling direct data mixing without modifying downstream policies. For locomotion, SuperSuit maps natural human stepping to continuous planar base velocities, eliminating discrete command switches. For manipulation, it employs a strictly isomorphic wearable arm in both modes, while policy training is formulated in a shift-invariant delta-joint representation to mitigate calibration offsets and structural compliance without inverse kinematics. Real-world experiments on long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks show 2.6$\times$ higher demonstration throughput in active mode compared to a teleoperation baseline, comparable policy performance when substituting teleoperation data with active demonstrations at fixed dataset size, and monotonic performance improvement as active data volume increases. These results indicate that consistent kinematic representations across collection modalities enable scalable data acquisition for long-horizon mobile manipulation.

</details>


### [200] [Open-Source Based and ETSI Compliant Cooperative, Connected, and Automated Mini-Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06343)
*Lorenzo Farina,Federico Gavioli,Salvatore Iandolo,Francesco Moretti,Giuseppe Perrone,Matteo Piccoli,Francesco Raviglione,Marco Rapelli,Antonio Solida,Paolo Burgio,Carlo Augusto Grazia,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出使用1:10比例缩小的协同、自主、连接迷你车作为从模拟到实际实验的中间步骤，降低测试成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车行业正从人工控制向完全自动化、连接和协同发展，但新算法的实地测试成本高昂，从模拟到实际实验需要巨大投资，可能成为障碍

Method: 开发1:10比例迷你车平台，配备Jetson Orin板运行ROS2实现自主操作，Raspberry Pi板运行开源OScar实现连接，OScar实现完整的ETSI C-ITS兼容协议栈

Result: 通过实施Day-1交叉路口碰撞预警应用案例，验证了平台的可行性和潜力

Conclusion: 提出的迷你车平台为协同智能交通系统算法测试提供了经济有效的中间步骤，降低了从模拟到实际实验的过渡成本

Abstract: The automotive sector is following a revolutionary path from vehicles controlled by humans to vehicles that will be fully automated, fully connected, and ultimately fully cooperative. Along this road, new cooperative algorithms and protocols will be designed and field tested, which represents a great challenge in terms of costs. In this context, in particular, moving from simulations to practical experiments requires huge investments that are not always affordable and may become a barrier in some cases. To solve this issue and provide the community with an intermediate step, we here propose the use of 1:10 scaled cooperative, autonomous, and connected mini-cars. The mini-car is equipped with a Jetson Orin board running the open Robot Operating System 2 (ROS2), sensors for autonomous operations, and a Raspberry Pi board for connectivity mounting the open source Open Stack for Car (OScar). A key aspect of the proposal is the use of OScar, which implements a full ETSI cooperative-intelligent transport systems (C-ITS) compliant stack. The feasibility and potential of the proposed platform is here demonstrated through the implementation of a case study where the Day-1 intersection collision warning (ICW) application is implemented and validated.

</details>


### [201] [Safe Consensus of Cooperative Manipulation with Hierarchical Event-Triggered Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06356)
*Simiao Zhuang,Bingkun Huang,Zewen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分布式控制框架，通过分层事件触发控制屏障函数实现多机械臂协同运输的安全共识协调


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机械臂协同运输重型或大型负载需要在协调编队跟踪的同时，在有限通信和实时计算预算下强制执行严格的安全约束

Method: 开发基于共识的协议实现任务空间平移和旋转一致性，提出三层分层事件触发安全架构，集成风险感知领导者选择和平滑切换策略

Result: 在真实硬件实验中验证了更高精度的协同操作，在严格安全约束下显著降低了计算成本和通信频率

Conclusion: 提出的分布式控制框架有效解决了多机械臂协同运输中的安全协调问题，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: Cooperative transport and manipulation of heavy or bulky payloads by multiple manipulators requires coordinated formation tracking, while simultaneously enforcing strict safety constraints in varying environments with limited communication and real-time computation budgets. This paper presents a distributed control framework that achieves consensus coordination with safety guarantees via hierarchical event-triggered control barrier functions (CBFs). We first develop a consensus-based protocol that relies solely on local neighbor information to enforce both translational and rotational consistency in task space. Building on this coordination layer, we propose a three-level hierarchical event-triggered safety architecture with CBFs, which is integrated with a risk-aware leader selection and smooth switching strategy to reduce online computation. The proposed approach is validated through real-world hardware experiments using two Franka manipulators operating with static obstacles, as well as comprehensive simulations demonstrating scalable multi-arm cooperation with dynamic obstacles. Results demonstrate higher precision cooperation under strict safety constraints, achieving substantially reduced computational cost and communication frequency compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [202] [Data Analogies Enable Efficient Cross-Embodiment Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06450)
*Jonathan Yang,Chelsea Finn,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探索了机器人演示数据的最佳组织形式，发现对于形态学差异，配对演示（数据类比）比简单扩大数据规模更有效，能显著提升跨平台迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略通常在不同机器人、场景和视角的演示数据上训练，但如何有效组织和扩展这种异构数据以真正提升目标场景性能仍不明确。本研究旨在探索什么形式的演示数据最能促进跨机器人设置的迁移。

Method: 通过控制实验，系统改变末端执行器形态、机器人平台外观和相机视角，比较单纯增加演示数量与不同方式扩展数据多样性的效果。模拟实验分析不同因素对迁移的影响，并将发现应用于真实世界场景。

Result: 模拟实验显示：感知变化（如视角）受益于广泛多样性，而形态学变化从非结构化多样性中获益较少，最大增益来自数据类比（即跨不同具身形式的配对演示）。基于此，仅通过改变数据组成，在真实世界中将跨具身迁移成功率平均提升了22.5%。

Conclusion: 对于跨机器人迁移，数据组成比单纯扩大规模更重要。针对形态学差异，配对演示（数据类比）是最有效的策略，这为构建更高效的机器人学习数据集提供了指导。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies are trained on demonstrations collected across a wide variety of robots, scenes, and viewpoints. Yet it remains unclear how to best organize and scale such heterogeneous data so that it genuinely improves performance in a given target setting. In this work, we ask: what form of demonstration data is most useful for enabling transfer across robot set-ups? We conduct controlled experiments that vary end-effector morphology, robot platform appearance, and camera perspective, and compare the effects of simply scaling the number of demonstrations against systematically broadening the diversity in different ways. Our simulated experiments show that while perceptual shifts such as viewpoint benefit most from broad diversity, morphology shifts benefit far less from unstructured diversity and instead see the largest gains from data analogies, i.e. paired demonstrations that align scenes, tasks, and/or trajectories across different embodiments. Informed by the simulation results, we improve real-world cross-embodiment transfer success by an average of $22.5\%$ over large-scale, unpaired datasets by changing only the composition of the data.

</details>


### [203] [History-Conditioned Spatio-Temporal Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06480)
*Qitong Wang,Yijun Liang,Ming Li,Tianyi Zhou,Christopher Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的时空视觉token剪枝框架，用于VLA模型的视觉语言导航，在保持导航精度的同时显著提升推理效率，实现实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言动作模型在导航任务中表现出色，但计算成本高、延迟大，限制了实时部署。需要一种高效的方法来减少冗余计算，同时保持导航性能。

Method: 提出无需训练的时空视觉token剪枝框架：1）对当前视图应用空间token选择；2）对历史记忆进行时空压缩；3）基于注意力的token重要性评估和查询引导的时空过滤；4）无需重新训练或修改预训练模型，即插即用。

Result: 在标准VLN基准测试中显著优于现有剪枝策略，在极端剪枝场景下仍能保持优越的导航精度，同时保持高度竞争力的推理效率。在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的实际部署验证了可靠的低延迟指令跟随导航。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于弥合大规模多模态建模与高效实时机器人导航部署之间的差距，为VLA模型的实时应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables robots to follow natural-language instructions in visually grounded environments, serving as a key capability for embodied robotic systems. Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong navigation performance, but their high computational cost introduces latency that limits real-time deployment. We propose a training-free spatio-temporal vision token pruning framework tailored to VLA-based VLN. We apply spatial token selection to the current view, alongside spatio-temporal compression for historical memories, enabling efficient long-horizon inference while reducing redundant computation. Leveraging attention-based token importance and query-guided spatio-temporal filtering, the proposed approach preserves navigation-relevant information without retraining or modifying pretrained models, allowing plug-and-play integration into existing VLA systems. Through experiments on standard VLN benchmarks, we confirm that our method significantly outperforms existing pruning strategies. It successfully preserves superior navigation accuracy under extreme pruning scenarios, all while maintaining the highly competitive inference efficiency. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates reliable and low-latency instruction-following navigation under practical robotic constraints. We hope this work helps bridge the gap between large-scale multimodal modeling and efficient, real-time embodied deployment in robotic navigation systems.

</details>


### [204] [Control Barrier Corridors: From Safety Functions to Safe Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06494)
*Ömür Arslan,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的控制屏障走廊概念，将控制屏障函数和安全运动走廊两种安全方法统一起来，通过将控制屏障函数转换为局部安全目标区域，用于反馈控制系统中的参考目标选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制屏障函数和安全运动走廊是机器人安全运动规划和控制的两种不同方法（功能性和几何性），但它们在技术上存在差异。本文旨在统一这两种方法，为机器人提供更有效的安全自主性保障。

Method: 提出控制屏障走廊的新概念，将控制屏障函数转换为局部安全目标区域，用于反馈控制系统的参考目标选择。通过凸屏障函数，将个体状态安全性扩展到控制屏障走廊上，前提是控制收敛速率与屏障衰减速率匹配。

Result: 展示了在完全驱动系统、运动学独轮车和线性输出调节系统上的应用示例，表明控制屏障走廊能够在系统运动期间实现安全可达的持续目标选择，并在未知环境自主探索中实现了可验证的安全持续路径跟随。

Conclusion: 控制屏障走廊统一了控制屏障函数和安全运动走廊两种方法，揭示了安全性和反应性之间的权衡关系。该方法能够在系统运动期间实现安全可达的持续目标选择，为机器人在非结构化复杂环境中的安全自主操作提供了新工具。

Abstract: Safe autonomy is a critical requirement and a key enabler for robots to operate safely in unstructured complex environments. Control barrier functions and safe motion corridors are two widely used but technically distinct safety methods, functional and geometric, respectively, for safe motion planning and control. Control barrier functions are applied to the safety filtering of control inputs to limit the decay rate of system safety, whereas safe motion corridors are geometrically constructed to define a local safe zone around the system state for use in motion optimization and reference-governor design. This paper introduces a new notion of control barrier corridors, which unifies these two approaches by converting control barrier functions into local safe goal regions for reference goal selection in feedback control systems. We show, with examples on fully actuated systems, kinematic unicycles, and linear output regulation systems, that individual state safety can be extended locally over control barrier corridors for convex barrier functions, provided the control convergence rate matches the barrier decay rate, highlighting a trade-off between safety and reactiveness. Such safe control barrier corridors enable safely reachable persistent goal selection over continuously changing barrier corridors during system motion, which we demonstrate for verifiably safe and persistent path following in autonomous exploration of unknown environments.

</details>


### [205] [A Unified Low-Dimensional Design Embedding for Joint Optimization of Shape, Material, and Actuation in Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06497)
*Vittorio Candiello,Manuel Mekkattu,Mike Y. Michelis,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于软体机器人协同设计的平滑低维嵌入表示，统一形状、材料和驱动参数，显著降低搜索空间维度并提升优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人的功能实现依赖于几何形状、材料组成和驱动的紧密耦合，传统方法难以同时优化这三个方面。非线性大变形力学增加了仿真成本，而接触、碰撞处理和非光滑状态转换限制了基于梯度方法的适用性。

Method: 引入平滑低维设计嵌入表示，通过连续变形映射参考几何形状，将材料属性编码为空间场，两者共享基函数构建。这种表示统一了形状变形、多材料分布和驱动参数，大幅降低搜索空间维度。

Result: 实验表明：1) 设计表达能力随基函数数量增加而增强；2) 联合优化形状、材料和驱动的效果优于顺序策略；3) 该方法与底层模拟器无关，兼容黑盒仿真流程；4) 在多个动态任务中超越神经网络和体素基线方法，且使用更少设计参数。

Conclusion: 通过结构化设计空间本身，能够实现软体机器人的高效协同设计，统一的嵌入表示在表达能力和优化效率方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Soft robots achieve functionality through tight coupling among geometry, material composition, and actuation. As a result, effective design optimization requires these three aspects to be considered jointly rather than in isolation. This coupling is computationally challenging: nonlinear large-deformation mechanics increase simulation cost, while contact, collision handling, and non-smooth state transitions limit the applicability of standard gradient-based approaches. We introduce a smooth, low-dimensional design embedding for soft robots that unifies shape morphing, multi-material distribution, and actuation within a single structured parameter space. Shape variation is modeled through continuous deformation maps of a reference geometry, while material properties are encoded as spatial fields. Both are constructed from shared basis functions. This representation enables expressive co-design while drastically reducing the dimensionality of the search space. In our experiments, we show that design expressiveness increases with the number of basis functions, unlike comparable neural network encodings whose representational capacity does not scale predictably with parameter count. We further show that joint co-optimization of shape, material, and actuation using our unified embedding consistently outperforms sequential strategies. All experiments are performed independently of the underlying simulator, confirming compatibility with black-box simulation pipelines. Across multiple dynamic tasks, the proposed embedding surpasses neural network and voxel-based baseline parameterizations while using significantly fewer design parameters. Together, these findings demonstrate that structuring the design space itself enables efficient co-design of soft robots.

</details>


### [206] [CFEAR-Teach-and-Repeat: Fast and Accurate Radar-only Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06501)
*Maximilian Hilger,Daniel Adolfsson,Ralf Becker,Henrik Andreasson,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CFEAR-TR：一种基于单旋转雷达的教与重复定位方法，用于恶劣天气下的自主导航，通过联合对齐实时扫描与存储扫描及滑动窗口关键帧，实现高精度鲁棒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在恶劣天气条件下，光学传感器可能失效，因此需要可靠的地图定位方法。雷达传感器在恶劣天气中表现更稳定，但现有雷达定位方法精度不足，需要缩小与激光雷达定位的差距。

Method: 使用单旋转雷达，采用教与重复定位框架。通过多普勒补偿测量计算稀疏定向表面点表示雷达扫描。定位时联合对齐实时扫描与存储的教阶段扫描以及滑动窗口中的实时关键帧。地图存储在姿态图中，定位时遍历该图。

Result: 在Boreas数据集的测试序列上，CFEAR-TR定位精度达到0.117米和0.096°，比现有最佳方法提升高达63%。运行效率为29Hz，大幅缩小了与激光雷达定位的差距，特别是在航向估计方面。

Conclusion: CFEAR-TR实现了恶劣天气下轻量级、鲁棒的雷达定位，精度接近激光雷达水平，为自主导航提供了可靠的解决方案。开源C++实现可供社区使用。

Abstract: Reliable localization in prior maps is essential for autonomous navigation, particularly under adverse weather, where optical sensors may fail. We present CFEAR-TR, a teach-and-repeat localization pipeline using a single spinning radar, which is designed for easily deployable, lightweight, and robust navigation in adverse conditions. Our method localizes by jointly aligning live scans to both stored scans from the teach mapping pass, and to a sliding window of recent live keyframes. This ensures accurate and robust pose estimation across different seasons and weather phenomena. Radar scans are represented using a sparse set of oriented surface points, computed from Doppler-compensated measurements. The map is stored in a pose graph that is traversed during localization. Experiments on the held-out test sequences from the Boreas dataset show that CFEAR-TR can localize with an accuracy as low as 0.117 m and 0.096°, corresponding to improvements of up to 63% over the previous state of the art, while running efficiently at 29 Hz. These results substantially narrow the gap to lidar-level localization, particularly in heading estimation. We make the C++ implementation of our work available to the community.

</details>


### [207] [SG-DOR: Learning Scene Graphs with Direction-Conditioned Occlusion Reasoning for Pepper Plants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06512)
*Rohit Menon,Niklas Mueller-Goldingen,Sicong Pan,Gokul Krishna Chenchani,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-DOR：一种用于密集作物冠层机器人采摘的关系框架，通过场景图编码物理附着和方向条件遮挡关系，为干预规划提供结构化信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集作物冠层中，机器人采摘需要有效的干预策略，这些策略不仅依赖于几何信息，还需要明确的方向条件遮挡关系来识别哪些器官遮挡了目标果实。

Method: 提出SG-DOR框架，基于实例分割的器官点云推断场景图，编码物理附着和方向条件遮挡关系。引入遮挡排序任务来检索和排序候选叶片，并提出方向感知图神经网络架构，具有每果实叶片集注意力和联合级聚合。

Result: 在多植物合成辣椒数据集上的实验显示，相比强消融模型，在遮挡预测（F1=0.73，NDCG@3=0.85）和附着推断（边F1=0.83）方面均有改进。

Conclusion: SG-DOR框架能够有效推断作物冠层中的关系结构，为下游干预规划提供有价值的结构化关系信号。

Abstract: Robotic harvesting in dense crop canopies requires effective interventions that depend not only on geometry, but also on explicit, direction-conditioned relations identifying which organs obstruct a target fruit. We present SG-DOR (Scene Graphs with Direction-Conditioned Occlusion Reasoning), a relational framework that, given instance-segmented organ point clouds, infers a scene graph encoding physical attachments and direction-conditioned occlusion. We introduce an occlusion ranking task for retrieving and ranking candidate leaves for a target fruit and approach direction, and propose a direction-aware graph neural architecture with per-fruit leaf-set attention and union-level aggregation. Experiments on a multi-plant synthetic pepper dataset show improved occlusion prediction (F1=0.73, NDCG@3=0.85) and attachment inference (edge F1=0.83) over strong ablations, yielding a structured relational signal for downstream intervention planning.

</details>


### [208] [Underactuated multimodal jumping robot for extraterrestrial exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06525)
*Neil R. Wagner,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 仅用三个执行器的欠驱动单足机器人，通过两个反应轮控制空间姿态，实现滚动、跳跃、着陆和自扶正等多模式运动，适用于低重力环境探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一个轻量、紧凑的机器人系统，能够在低重力天体（如小行星、月球）上实现多模式运动，以适应不同的地形和环境条件，提高探索能力。

Method: 使用仅有两个反应轮和三个执行器的欠驱动单足机器人设计。采用两种控制器：平衡控制器（用于地面跳跃方向瞄准）和空中重定向控制器（用于飞行后着陆腿瞄准）。通过简单切换运动模式来适应不同地形。

Result: 成功演示了滚动、目标跳跃和着陆、自扶正等功能。系统尺寸仅为0.33米，重量1.25公斤，仅用三个执行器就实现了多模式运动能力。

Conclusion: 这种简单的欠驱动设计能够在低重力环境中实现有效的多模式运动，为天体探索提供了轻量、紧凑的机器人解决方案，展示了通过最小化执行器数量实现复杂运动能力的可行性。

Abstract: We present a rolling and jumping underactuated monopedal robot designed to explore multimodal locomotion on low-gravity bodies. It uses only two reaction wheels to control its spatial orientation with two controllers: a balancing controller which can aim the robot's jump direction on the ground, and an aerial reorientation controller which can aim the robot's leg for landing after flight. We demonstrate rolling, targeted jumping and landing, and self-righting using only three actuators total, keeping system size to 0.33m and 1.25kg. Simple switching between locomotion modes enables the system to deal with differing landscapes and environmental conditions.

</details>


### [209] [Unified Learning of Temporal Task Structure and Action Timing for Bimanual Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06538)
*Christian Dreher,Patrick Dormanns,Andre Meixner,Tamim Asfour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从人类演示中同时学习符号和亚符号时间任务约束的方法，用于生成可执行的双手机器人操作计划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将高层任务规划和低层运动同步分开处理，导致符号时间关系（高层推理）和具体时间参数（执行协调）之间存在鸿沟，无法有效支持双手机器人操作

Method: 1) 使用三维时间表示和多元高斯混合模型学习动作间的亚符号时间关系；2) 基于DPLL算法寻找并排序所有无矛盾的Allen关系分配，表示任务的不同模式；3) 结合符号和亚符号约束，通过优化规划系统生成时间参数化计划

Result: 在多个数据集上评估，该方法生成的时间参数化计划比最具代表性的演示基线更接近人类演示

Conclusion: 成功填补了高层任务规划和低层运动同步之间的鸿沟，实现了从人类演示中学习符号和亚符号时间约束，并生成可执行的双手机器人操作计划

Abstract: Temporal task structure is fundamental for bimanual manipulation: a robot must not only know that one action precedes or overlaps another, but also when each action should occur and how long it should take. While symbolic temporal relations enable high-level reasoning about task structure and alternative execution sequences, concrete timing parameters are equally essential for coordinating two hands at the execution level. Existing approaches address these two levels in isolation, leaving a gap between high-level task planning and low-level movement synchronization. This work presents an approach for learning both symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints from human demonstrations and deriving executable, temporally parametrized plans for bimanual manipulation. Our contributions are (i) a 3-dimensional representation of timings between two actions with methods based on multivariate Gaussian Mixture Models to represent temporal relationships between actions on a subsymbolic level, (ii) a method based on the Davis-Putnam-Logemann-Loveland (DPLL) algorithm that finds and ranks all contradiction-free assignments of Allen relations to action pairs, representing different modes of a task, and (iii) an optimization-based planning system that combines the identified symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints to derive temporally parametrized plans for robot execution. We evaluate our approach on several datasets, demonstrating that our method generates temporally parametrized plans closer to human demonstrations than the most characteristic demonstration baseline.

</details>


### [210] [Uncertainty-Aware Adaptive Dynamics For Underwater Vehicle-Manipulator Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06548)
*Edward Morgan,Nenyi K Dadson,Corina Barbalata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于水下车辆-机械臂系统的不确定性感知自适应动力学模型框架，该框架保持线性参数结构，嵌入凸物理一致性约束，并采用移动视界估计进行在线参数估计和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下车辆-机械臂系统受时变水动力效应影响，需要准确的自适应动态模型。现有方法在参数估计中缺乏物理一致性约束和不确定性量化，限制了模型的可靠性和控制性能。

Method: 提出不确定性感知自适应动力学模型框架：1）保持车辆和机械臂集总参数的线性结构；2）嵌入凸物理一致性约束（惯性、阻尼、摩擦、静水力学）；3）采用移动视界估计堆叠历史回归器并量化参数演化不确定性。

Result: 在BlueROV2 Heavy和4自由度机械臂上的实验表明：机械臂拟合R²=0.88-0.98，斜率接近1；车辆纵荡、垂荡和横摇在强耦合和噪声下良好再现；中值求解时间约0.023秒/更新；相比固定参数模型，各自由度MAE和RMSE一致降低；参数物理合理，置信区间覆盖率近100%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在线估计物理一致的参数并量化不确定性，实现快速收敛和校准预测，为水下环境中的可靠前馈控制和仿真提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate and adaptive dynamic models are critical for underwater vehicle-manipulator systems where hydrodynamic effects induce time-varying parameters. This paper introduces a novel uncertainty-aware adaptive dynamics model framework that remains linear in lumped vehicle and manipulator parameters, and embeds convex physical consistency constraints during online estimation. Moving horizon estimation is used to stack horizon regressors, enforce realizable inertia, damping, friction, and hydrostatics, and quantify uncertainty from parameter evolution. Experiments on a BlueROV2 Heavy with a 4-DOF manipulator demonstrate rapid convergence and calibrated predictions. Manipulator fits achieve R2 = 0.88 to 0.98 with slopes near unity, while vehicle surge, heave, and roll are reproduced with good fidelity under stronger coupling and noise. Median solver time is approximately 0.023 s per update, confirming online feasibility. A comparison against a fixed parameter model shows consistent reductions in MAE and RMSE across degrees of freedom. Results indicate physically plausible parameters and confidence intervals with near 100% coverage, enabling reliable feedforward control and simulation in underwater environments.

</details>


### [211] [Fly360: Omnidirectional Obstacle Avoidance within Drone View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06573)
*Xiangkai Zhang,Dizhe Zhang,WenZhuo Cao,Zhaoliang Wan,Yingjie Niu,Lu Qi,Xu Yang,Zhiyong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fly360是一个用于全景无人机全向避障的两阶段感知-决策系统，通过固定随机偏航训练策略实现从任意方向的稳定避障


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无人机避障方法主要依赖有限视场传感器，不适合需要全空间感知的场景，特别是当运动方向与无人机航向不一致时。这促使研究者探索具有全视角感知的全景无人机全向避障

Method: 提出Fly360两阶段感知-决策流水线：感知阶段将全景RGB观测转换为深度图作为鲁棒中间表示；决策阶段使用轻量级策略网络从深度输入输出机体坐标系速度命令。采用固定随机偏航训练策略

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，Fly360实现了稳定的全向避障，在所有任务中都优于前向视角基线方法

Conclusion: Fly360成功解决了全景无人机全向避障问题，为无人机在复杂环境中从任意方向进行避障提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Obstacle avoidance in unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), as a fundamental capability, has gained increasing attention with the growing focus on spatial intelligence. However, current obstacle-avoidance methods mainly depend on limited field-of-view sensors and are ill-suited for UAV scenarios which require full-spatial awareness when the movement direction differs from the UAV's heading. This limitation motivates us to explore omnidirectional obstacle avoidance for panoramic drones with full-view perception. We first study an under explored problem setting in which a UAV must generate collision-free motion in environments with obstacles from arbitrary directions, and then construct a benchmark that consists of three representative flight tasks. Based on such settings, we propose Fly360, a two-stage perception-decision pipeline with a fixed random-yaw training strategy. At the perception stage, panoramic RGB observations are input and converted into depth maps as a robust intermediate representation. For the policy network, it is lightweight and used to output body-frame velocity commands from depth inputs. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Fly360 achieves stable omnidirectional obstacle avoidance and outperforms forward-view baselines across all tasks. Our model is available at https://zxkai.github.io/fly360/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [RoboLayout: Differentiable 3D Scene Generation for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05522)
*Ali Shamsaddinlou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoboLayout扩展了LayoutVLM，通过集成可达性约束和局部优化改进，生成适合不同具身智能体交互的室内场景布局


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在生成语义连贯的3D场景布局方面取得进展，但生成的布局往往难以满足具身智能体的实际交互需求，特别是在物理约束的室内环境中

Method: 1. 在可微分布局优化过程中集成显式的可达性约束；2. 引入智能体抽象表示，支持不同物理能力的实体（机器人、人类、动物等）；3. 提出局部细化阶段，选择性重新优化有问题的物体放置，提高收敛效率

Result: RoboLayout保持了LayoutVLM的语义对齐和物理合理性，同时增强了面向智能体的室内场景生成适用性，在不同场景配置中表现出色

Conclusion: RoboLayout通过智能体感知推理和优化稳定性改进，成功解决了具身智能体在物理约束环境中交互可行性问题，为面向特定智能体的环境设计提供了灵活框架

Abstract: Recent advances in vision language models (VLMs) have shown strong potential for spatial reasoning and 3D scene layout generation from open-ended language instructions. However, generating layouts that are not only semantically coherent but also feasible for interaction by embodied agents remains challenging, particularly in physically constrained indoor environments. In this paper, RoboLayout is introduced as an extension of LayoutVLM that augments the original framework with agent-aware reasoning and improved optimization stability. RoboLayout integrates explicit reachability constraints into a differentiable layout optimization process, enabling the generation of layouts that are navigable and actionable by embodied agents. Importantly, the agent abstraction is not limited to a specific robot platform and can represent diverse entities with distinct physical capabilities, such as service robots, warehouse robots, humans of different age groups, or animals, allowing environment design to be tailored to the intended agent. In addition, a local refinement stage is proposed that selectively reoptimizes problematic object placements while keeping the remainder of the scene fixed, improving convergence efficiency without increasing global optimization iterations. Overall, RoboLayout preserves the strong semantic alignment and physical plausibility of LayoutVLM while enhancing applicability to agent-centric indoor scene generation, as demonstrated by experimental results across diverse scene configurations.

</details>


### [213] [Real-Time AI Service Economy: A Framework for Agentic Computing Across the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05614)
*Lauri Lovén,Alaa Saleh,Reza Farahani,Ilir Murturi,Miguel Bordallo López,Praveen Kumar Donta,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了在设备-边缘-云连续体中，服务依赖图结构如何影响去中心化价格资源分配的稳定性，发现层次化依赖图能实现稳定均衡，而复杂依赖图会导致价格振荡，并提出混合管理架构来解决这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着实时AI服务在设备-边缘-云连续体上运行，自主AI代理生成延迟敏感工作负载，协调多阶段处理流水线，并在策略和治理约束下竞争共享资源。需要理解去中心化价格资源分配在何种条件下能可靠地大规模工作。

Method: 将服务依赖图建模为DAG（节点表示计算阶段，边编码执行顺序），分析不同拓扑结构对价格稳定性的影响。提出混合管理架构，通过跨域集成器将复杂子图封装为资源切片，向市场提供更简单、结构良好的接口。进行系统消融研究（6个实验，1,620次运行，每个10个种子）。

Result: 1) 依赖图拓扑是价格稳定性和可扩展性的首要决定因素；2) 混合架构在不牺牲吞吐量的情况下将价格波动降低70-75%；3) 治理约束产生可量化的效率-合规权衡，取决于拓扑和负载；4) 在真实出价下，去中心化市场与集中化价值最优基线匹配。

Conclusion: 服务依赖图结构是去中心化价格资源分配能否可靠工作的关键因素。层次化依赖图支持稳定均衡和高效分配，而复杂依赖图需要混合架构来封装复杂性。去中心化协调可以复制集中化分配质量，但需要适当的机制设计和架构支持。

Abstract: Real-time AI services increasingly operate across the device-edge-cloud continuum, where autonomous AI agents generate latency-sensitive workloads, orchestrate multi-stage processing pipelines, and compete for shared resources under policy and governance constraints. This article shows that the structure of service-dependency graphs, modelled as DAGs whose nodes represent compute stages and whose edges encode execution ordering, is a primary determinant of whether decentralised, price-based resource allocation can work reliably at scale. When dependency graphs are hierarchical (tree or series-parallel), prices converge to stable equilibria, optimal allocations can be computed efficiently, and under appropriate mechanism design (with quasilinear utilities and discrete slice items), agents have no incentive to misreport their valuations within each decision epoch. When dependencies are more complex, with cross-cutting ties between pipeline stages, prices oscillate, allocation quality degrades, and the system becomes difficult to manage. To bridge this gap, we propose a hybrid management architecture in which cross-domain integrators encapsulate complex sub-graphs into resource slices that present a simpler, well-structured interface to the rest of the market. A systematic ablation study across six experiments (1,620 runs, 10 seeds each) confirms that (i) dependency-graph topology is a first-order determinant of price stability and scalability,(ii) the hybrid architecture reduces price volatility by up to 70-75% without sacrificing throughput, (iii) governance constraints create quantifiable efficiency-compliance trade-offs that depend jointly on topology and load, and (iv) under truthful bidding the decentralised market matches a centralised value-optimal baseline, confirming that decentralised coordination can replicate centralised allocation quality.

</details>


### [214] [Reasoning Models Struggle to Control their Chains of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05706)
*Chen Yueh-Han,Robert McCarthy,Bruce W. Lee,He He,Ian Kivlichan,Bowen Baker,Micah Carroll,Tomek Korbak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CoT-Control评估套件，发现推理模型对思维链的控制能力远低于对最终输出的控制能力，这为思维链监控提供了谨慎乐观的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如果模型能够控制其在思维链中表达的内容，可能会破坏思维链监控的有效性。需要评估这种"思维链可控性"能力，以了解思维链监控的潜在风险。

Method: 引入CoT-Control评估套件，包含要求模型在遵循特定思维链指令下解决问题的任务（如回答遗传学问题时不使用"染色体"一词）。评估不同模型、训练方式、计算资源和任务难度下的可控性表现。

Result: 推理模型的思维链可控性显著低于输出可控性（Claude Sonnet 4.5仅2.7% vs 61.9%）。可控性随模型规模增大而提高，但随RL训练、测试时计算和问题难度增加而降低。即使给予激励或对抗性提示优化，可控性也没有显著提升。

Conclusion: 目前思维链可控性不太可能成为思维链监控的失效模式，但机制尚不清楚。建议前沿实验室在未来模型中跟踪思维链可控性，以维护思维链监控的有效性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising tool for detecting misbehaviors and understanding the motivations of modern reasoning models. However, if models can control what they verbalize in their CoT, it could undermine CoT monitorability. To measure this undesirable capability -- CoT controllability -- we introduce the CoT-Control evaluation suite, which includes tasks that require models to solve problems while adhering to CoT instructions, e.g., reasoning about a genetics question without using the word 'chromosome'. We show that reasoning models possess significantly lower CoT controllability than output controllability; for instance, Claude Sonnet 4.5 can control its CoT only 2.7% of the time but 61.9% when controlling its final output. We also find that CoT controllability is higher for larger models and decreases with more RL training, test-time compute, and increased problem difficulty. CoT controllability failures extend even to situations in which models are given incentives (as opposed to direct requests) to evade CoT monitors, although models exhibit slightly higher controllability when they are told they are being monitored. Similarly, eliciting controllability by adversarially optimizing prompts does not meaningfully increase controllability. Our results leave us cautiously optimistic that CoT controllability is currently unlikely to be a failure mode of CoT monitorability. However, the mechanism behind low controllability is not well understood. Given its importance for maintaining CoT monitorability, we recommend that frontier labs track CoT controllability in future models.

</details>


### [215] [Evolving Medical Imaging Agents via Experience-driven Self-skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05860)
*Lin Fan,Pengyu Dai,Zhipeng Deng,Haolin Wang,Xun Gong,Yefeng Zheng,Yafei Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MACRO：一种自演化的医学智能体，通过从已验证的执行轨迹中自主发现有效的多步骤工具序列，将其合成为可复用的复合工具，实现从静态工具组合到经验驱动工具发现的转变，提升多步骤编排精度和跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床图像解读本质上是多步骤和工具中心的，现有LLM智能体将工具集和调用策略视为静态部署，这种设计在真实世界领域转移、跨任务和不断演化的诊断需求下显得脆弱，预定义工具链经常退化并需要昂贵的手动重新设计。

Method: 从已验证的执行轨迹中自主识别重复出现的有效多步骤工具序列，将其合成为可复用的复合工具，并注册为新的高级原语；采用轻量级图像特征记忆将工具选择基于视觉-临床上下文，通过GRPO-like训练循环强化可靠调用发现的复合工具，实现闭环自改进。

Result: 在多样化的医学影像数据集和任务上的广泛实验表明，自主复合工具发现持续提高了多步骤编排精度和跨领域泛化能力，超越了强基线方法和近期最先进的智能体方法。

Conclusion: MACRO通过经验驱动的工具发现，弥合了脆弱静态工具使用与自适应、上下文感知的临床AI辅助之间的差距，实现了从静态工具组合到自演化、经验增强的医学智能体的转变。

Abstract: Clinical image interpretation is inherently multi-step and tool-centric: clinicians iteratively combine visual evidence with patient context, quantify findings, and refine their decisions through a sequence of specialized procedures. While LLM-based agents promise to orchestrate such heterogeneous medical tools, existing systems treat tool sets and invocation strategies as static after deployment. This design is brittle under real-world domain shifts, across tasks, and evolving diagnostic requirements, where predefined tool chains frequently degrade and demand costly manual re-design. We propose MACRO, a self-evolving, experience-augmented medical agent that shifts from static tool composition to experience-driven tool discovery. From verified execution trajectories, the agent autonomously identifies recurring effective multi-step tool sequences, synthesizes them into reusable composite tools, and registers these as new high-level primitives that continuously expand its behavioral repertoire. A lightweight image-feature memory grounds tool selection in a visual-clinical context, while a GRPO-like training loop reinforces reliable invocation of discovered composites, enabling closed-loop self-improvement with minimal supervision. Extensive experiments across diverse medical imaging datasets and tasks demonstrate that autonomous composite tool discovery consistently improves multi-step orchestration accuracy and cross-domain generalization over strong baselines and recent state-of-the-art agentic methods, bridging the gap between brittle static tool use and adaptive, context-aware clinical AI assistance. Code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [216] [The World Won't Stay Still: Programmable Evolution for Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05910)
*Guangrui Li,Yaochen Xie,Yi Liu,Ziwei Dong,Xingyuan Pan,Tianqi Zheng,Jason Choi,Michael J. Morais,Binit Jha,Shaunak Mishra,Bingrou Zhou,Chen Luo,Monica Xiao Cheng,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProEvolve是一个基于图的框架，用于以可编程方式演化LLM智能体环境，通过图变换生成动态环境来评估智能体对现实世界变化的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试大多假设静态环境，忽略了现实世界的演化特性和智能体对环境变化的鲁棒性。需要一种可扩展且可控的环境演化方法来更好地评估智能体对现实世界动态的适应性。

Method: 提出ProEvolve框架，使用类型化关系图统一表示环境的数据、工具和模式。通过图变换（添加、删除、修改）来编程化地演化环境，并通过子图采样和编程实例化任务沙箱。

Result: 将单个环境演化成200个环境和3,000个任务沙箱，并据此对代表性智能体进行基准测试，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: ProEvolve提供了一种可编程的环境演化框架，能够生成动态环境来更真实地评估LLM智能体对现实世界变化的适应性，解决了现有基准测试的局限性。

Abstract: LLM-powered agents fulfill user requests by interacting with environments, querying data, and invoking tools in a multi-turn process. Yet, most existing benchmarks assume static environments with fixed schemas and toolsets, neglecting the evolutionary nature of the real world and agents' robustness to environmental changes. In this paper, we study a crucial problem: how to evolve the agent environment in a scalable and controllable way, thereby better evaluating agents' adaptability to real-world dynamics. We propose ProEvolve, a graph-based framework that makes environment evolution programmable. At its core, a typed relational graph provides a unified, explicit representation of the environment: data, tools, and schema. Under this formalism, adding, removing, or modifying capabilities are expressed as graph transformations that coherently propagate updates across tools, schemas, and data access. Building on this, ProEvolve can (1) program the evolutionary dynamics as graph transformations to generate environments automatically, and (2) instantiate task sandboxes via subgraph sampling and programming. We validate ProEvolve by evolving a single environment into 200 environments and 3,000 task sandboxes, and benchmark representative agents accordingly.

</details>


### [217] [DeepFact: Co-Evolving Benchmarks and Agents for Deep Research Factuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05912)
*Yukun Huang,Leonardo F. R. Ribeiro,Momchil Hardalov,Bhuwan Dhingra,Markus Dreyer,Venkatesh Saligrama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AtS方法构建可演进的DRR事实性基准，创建了DeepFact-Bench基准和DeepFact-Eval验证代理，显著提升专家标注准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强的LLM代理能生成深度研究报告，但验证声明级事实性仍具挑战。现有事实检查器主要针对通用领域的事实型原子声明，缺乏测试这些验证器是否适用于DRR的基准。构建此类基准本身就很困难，专家一次性标注准确率仅60.8%。

Method: 提出Evolving Benchmarking via Audit-then-Score (AtS)方法：基准标签和理由可明确修订。当验证器与当前基准不一致时，必须提交证据；审计员裁决争议；接受的修订在模型评分前更新基准。基于AtS创建了DeepFact-Bench（版本化DRR事实性基准）和DeepFact-Eval（文档级验证代理）。

Result: 经过四轮AtS迭代，专家微金标准确率从60.8%提升至90.9%，表明专家作为审计员比一次性标注员更可靠。DeepFact-Eval在DeepFact-Bench上优于现有验证器，并能很好地迁移到外部事实性数据集。

Conclusion: AtS方法通过可修订的基准构建显著提升了DRR事实性评估的可靠性，专家在审计角色中表现更佳。DeepFact-Bench和DeepFact-Eval为DRR事实性验证提供了有效的基准和工具。

Abstract: Search-augmented LLM agents can produce deep research reports (DRRs), but verifying claim-level factuality remains challenging. Existing fact-checkers are primarily designed for general-domain, factoid-style atomic claims, and there is no benchmark to test whether such verifiers transfer to DRRs. Yet building such a benchmark is itself difficult. We first show that static expert-labeled benchmarks are brittle in this setting: in a controlled study with PhD-level specialists, unassisted experts achieve only 60.8% accuracy on a hidden micro-gold set of verifiable claims. We propose Evolving Benchmarking via Audit-then-Score (AtS), where benchmark labels and rationales are explicitly revisable: when a verifier disagrees with the current benchmark, it must submit evidence; an auditor adjudicates the dispute; and accepted revisions update the benchmark before models are scored. Across four AtS rounds, expert micro-gold accuracy rises to 90.9%, indicating experts are substantially more reliable as auditors than as one-shot labelers. We instantiate AtS as DeepFact-Bench, a versioned DRR factuality benchmark with auditable rationales, and DeepFact-Eval, a document-level verification agent (with a grouped lite variant) that outperforms existing verifiers on DeepFact-Bench and transfers well to external factuality datasets.

</details>


### [218] [An Interactive Multi-Agent System for Evaluation of New Product Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05980)
*Bin Xuan,Ruo Ai,Hakyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于大语言模型的多智能体系统，用于自动化产品概念评估，通过八个虚拟专家代理进行技术可行性和市场可行性评估，结果与行业专家评估一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统专家主导的产品概念评估方法存在主观偏见、时间和成本高等局限性，需要更客观、高效的自动化评估方法来支持企业战略资源分配和项目决策。

Method: 构建基于大语言模型的多智能体系统，包含八个代表不同专业领域（如研发、营销）的虚拟代理，使用检索增强生成和实时搜索工具收集客观证据，通过结构化审议基于技术可行性和市场可行性两个维度评估产品概念，并利用专业产品评审数据进行微调。

Result: 在专业显示监视器概念的案例研究中，系统评估排名与资深行业专家的评估结果一致，验证了该多智能体评估方法的可用性。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统能够有效支持产品开发决策，提供客观、高效的产品概念评估，可作为传统专家评估的补充或替代方案。

Abstract: Product concept evaluation is a critical stage that determines strategic resource allocation and project success in enterprises. However, traditional expert-led approaches face limitations such as subjective bias and high time and cost requirements. To support this process, this study proposes an automated approach utilizing a large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent system (MAS). Through a systematic analysis of previous research on product development and team collaboration, this study established two primary evaluation dimensions, namely technical feasibility and market feasibility. The proposed system consists of a team of eight virtual agents representing specialized domains such as R&D and marketing. These agents use retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and real-time search tools to gather objective evidence and validate concepts through structured deliberations based on the established criteria. The agents were further fine-tuned using professional product review data to enhance their judgment accuracy. A case study involving professional display monitor concepts demonstrated that the system's evaluation rankings were consistent with those of senior industry experts. These results confirm the usability of the proposed multi-agent-based evaluation approach for supporting product development decisions.

</details>


### [219] [Agentic LLM Planning via Step-Wise PDDL Simulation: An Empirical Characterisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06064)
*Kai Göbel,Pierrick Lorang,Patrik Zips,Tobias Glück*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PyPDDLEngine，一个通过MCP接口将PDDL规划操作暴露为LLM工具调用的开源仿真引擎，评估了LLM作为交互式搜索策略在任务规划中的表现，发现代理式LLM规划相比直接规划仅有微小优势，且成本显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型（LLM）是否能够作为可行的规划器与经典符号方法相媲美，特别是在任务规划这一机器人自主系统的核心能力方面。研究LLM作为交互式搜索策略而非一次性生成完整行动序列的潜力。

Method: 开发PyPDDLEngine，一个开源的PDDL仿真引擎，通过Model Context Protocol（MCP）接口将规划操作暴露为LLM工具调用。LLM作为交互式搜索策略，每次选择一个行动，观察结果状态，并可重置重试。在102个国际规划竞赛（IPC）Blocksworld实例上评估四种方法：Fast Downward lama-first和seq-sat-lama-2011作为经典基线，直接LLM规划（Claude Haiku 4.5），以及通过PyPDDLEngine的代理式LLM规划。

Result: Fast Downward达到85.3%成功率。直接和代理式LLM方法分别达到63.7%和66.7%，代理式方法仅有3个百分点的微小优势，但每个解决方案的token成本高出5.7倍。在大多数共同解决的难度块中，两种LLM方法产生的计划比seq-sat-lama-2011更短，尽管后者有迭代质量改进。

Conclusion: 代理式LLM规划在PDDL任务中仅有微小优势，成本显著更高。LLM可能更多依赖训练数据回忆而非通用规划能力。代理式方法的收益取决于环境反馈的性质：编码代理受益于编译器错误和测试失败等外部验证信号，而PDDL步骤反馈是自我评估的，缺乏外部验证。

Abstract: Task planning, the problem of sequencing actions to reach a goal from an initial state, is a core capability requirement for autonomous robotic systems. Whether large language models (LLMs) can serve as viable planners alongside classical symbolic methods remains an open question. We present PyPDDLEngine, an open-source Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) simulation engine that exposes planning operations as LLM tool calls through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) interface. Rather than committing to a complete action sequence upfront, the LLM acts as an interactive search policy that selects one action at a time, observes each resulting state, and can reset and retry. We evaluate four approaches on 102 International Planning Competition (IPC) Blocksworld instances under a uniform 180-second budget: Fast Downward lama-first and seq-sat-lama-2011 as classical baselines, direct LLM planning (Claude Haiku 4.5), and agentic LLM planning via PyPDDLEngine. Fast Downward achieves 85.3% success. The direct and agentic LLM approaches achieve 63.7% and 66.7%, respectively, a consistent but modest three-percentage-point advantage for the agentic approach at $5.7\times$ higher token cost per solution. Across most co-solved difficulty blocks, both LLM approaches produce shorter plans than seq-sat-lama-2011 despite its iterative quality improvement, a result consistent with training-data recall rather than generalisable planning. These results suggest that agentic gains depend on the nature of environmental feedback. Coding agents benefit from externally grounded signals such as compiler errors and test failures, whereas PDDL step feedback is self-assessed, leaving the agent to evaluate its own progress without external verification.

</details>


### [220] [Aggregative Semantics for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06067)
*Yann Munro,Isabelle Bloch,Marie-Jeanne Lesot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的聚合语义家族，用于定量双极论证框架，通过将攻击者和支持者分开聚合的三阶段计算，增强了参数化和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在定量双极论证框架中，现有方法通常对称处理攻击和支持关系，但实际情境中攻击者和支持者可能扮演不对称角色。需要一种更灵活、可解释的语义来更好地建模这种不对称性。

Method: 提出聚合语义家族，采用三阶段计算：1) 单独计算攻击者的全局权重；2) 单独计算支持者的全局权重；3) 将这两个值与论证的内在权重聚合。讨论了三种聚合函数在不同情境下应满足的性质。

Result: 通过简单示例和最终测试500种聚合语义的案例，展示了聚合语义的广泛行为范围。三阶段分解使计算更具参数化，保持了双极性，并提高了语义的可理解性。

Conclusion: 聚合语义提供了一种新颖且更灵活的定量双极论证框架处理方法，通过分离攻击者和支持者的聚合，增强了语义的参数化能力和可解释性，为建模不对称论证关系提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Formal argumentation is being used increasingly in artificial intelligence as an effective and understandable way to model potentially conflicting pieces of information, called arguments, and identify so-called acceptable arguments depending on a chosen semantics. This paper deals with the specific context of Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAF), where arguments have intrinsic weights and can attack or support each other. In this context, we introduce a novel family of gradual semantics, called aggregative semantics. In order to deal with situations in which attackers and supporters do not play a symmetric role, and in contrast to modular semantics, we propose to aggregate attackers and supporters separately. This leads to a three-stage computation, which consists in computing a global weight for attackers and another for supporters, before aggregating these two values with the intrinsic weight of the argument. We discuss the properties that the three aggregation functions should satisfy depending on the context, as well as their relationships with the classical principles for gradual semantics. This discussion is supported by various simple examples, as well as a final example on which five hundred aggregative semantics are tested and compared, illustrating the range of possible behaviours with aggregative semantics. Decomposing the computation into three distinct and interpretable steps leads to a more parametrisable computation: it keeps the bipolarity one step further than what is done in the literature, and it leads to more understandable gradual semantics.

</details>


### [221] [Offline Materials Optimization with CliqueFlowmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06082)
*Jakub Grudzien Kuba,Benjamin Kurt Miller,Sergey Levine,Pieter Abbeel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CliqueFlowmer：一种基于离线模型优化的材料发现新方法，通过结合团簇优化与Transformer/流生成，直接优化目标材料性能，优于传统生成方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的材料计算方法中，生成模型因最大似然训练而无法有效探索材料空间的有利区域，需要一种能直接优化目标性能的替代方法

Method: 提出CliqueFlowmer模型，将团簇基模型优化（MBO）的最新进展融入Transformer和流生成中，实现离线模型优化与材料生成的融合

Result: CliqueFlowmer在优化能力上表现优异，其生成的材料性能显著优于传统生成基线方法

Conclusion: CliqueFlowmer为计算材料发现提供了一种有效的替代方法，通过开源代码支持专业材料优化问题和跨学科研究

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning inspired neural network-based approaches to computational materials discovery (CMD). A plethora of problems in this field involve finding materials that optimize a target property. Nevertheless, the increasingly popular generative modeling methods are ineffective at boldly exploring attractive regions of the materials space due to their maximum likelihood training. In this work, we offer an alternative CMD technique based on offline model-based optimization (MBO) that fuses direct optimization of a target material property into generation. To that end, we introduce a domain-specific model, dubbed CliqueFlowmer, that incorporates recent advances of clique-based MBO into transformer and flow generation. We validate CliqueFlowmer's optimization abilities and show that materials it produces strongly outperform those provided by generative baselines. To enable employment of CliqueFlowmer in specialized materials optimization problems and support interdisciplinary research, we open-source our code at https://github.com/znowu/CliqueFlowmer.

</details>


### [222] [Conversational Demand Response: Bidirectional Aggregator-Prosumer Coordination through Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06217)
*Reda El Makroum,Sebastian Zwickl-Bernhard,Lukas Kranzl,Hans Auer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对话式需求响应（CDR），通过双向自然语言交互协调聚合商和产消者，使用多智能体架构实现透明、可解释的需求响应协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求响应协调要么完全自动化，要么仅限于单向调度信号和价格警报，缺乏知情决策的可能性，难以维持产消者的持续参与。

Method: 采用双层多智能体架构：聚合商智能体调度灵活性请求，产消者家庭能源管理系统通过调用基于优化的工具评估可交付性和成本效益；支持产消者发起的上游通信。

Result: 概念验证评估显示交互在12秒内完成；系统展示了智能体AI如何弥合聚合商-产消者协调差距，在保持自动化需求响应可扩展性的同时，提供透明度、可解释性和用户自主权。

Conclusion: CDR通过双向自然语言交互实现了更有效的需求响应协调，所有系统组件已开源发布，支持可重复性和进一步开发。

Abstract: Residential demand response depends on sustained prosumer participation, yet existing coordination is either fully automated, or limited to one-way dispatch signals and price alerts that offer little possibility for informed decision-making. This paper introduces Conversational Demand Response (CDR), a coordination mechanism where aggregators and prosumers interact through bidirectional natural language, enabled through agentic AI. A two-tier multi-agent architecture is developed in which an aggregator agent dispatches flexibility requests and a prosumer Home Energy Management System (HEMS) assesses deliverability and cost-benefit by calling an optimization-based tool. CDR also enables prosumer-initiated upstream communication, where changes in preferences can reach the aggregator directly. Proof-of-concept evaluation shows that interactions complete in under 12 seconds. The architecture illustrates how agentic AI can bridge the aggregator-prosumer coordination gap, providing the scalability of automated DR while preserving the transparency, explainability, and user agency necessary for sustained prosumer participation. All system components, including agent prompts, orchestration logic, and simulation interfaces, are released as open source to enable reproducibility and further development.

</details>


### [223] [Artificial Intelligence for Climate Adaptation: Reinforcement Learning for Climate Change-Resilient Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06278)
*Miguel Costa,Arthur Vandervoort,Carolin Schmidt,João Miranda,Morten W. Petersen,Martin Drews,Karyn Morrisey,Francisco C. Pereira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的决策支持框架，用于长期洪水适应规划，结合降雨预测、洪水建模、交通模拟和影响量化，在哥本哈根案例中优于传统优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化加剧降雨和洪水，导致城市交通系统中断增加。设计有效适应策略面临挑战：长期基础设施投资、深度气候不确定性、洪水-基础设施-移动性复杂交互。

Method: 提出强化学习决策支持框架，作为综合评估模型，整合降雨预测、洪水建模、交通模拟，量化基础设施和移动性的直接间接影响。RL方法学习平衡投资维护成本与避免影响的适应策略。

Result: 在哥本哈根市区2024-2100年案例研究中，框架优于传统优化方法，发现协调的空间和时间适应路径，学习影响减少与适应投资之间的权衡，产生更具韧性的策略。

Conclusion: 强化学习作为灵活决策支持工具，在气候不确定性下进行适应性基础设施规划具有潜力，能发现协调的时空适应路径并学习成本效益权衡。

Abstract: Climate change is expected to intensify rainfall and, consequently, pluvial flooding, leading to increased disruptions in urban transportation systems over the coming decades. Designing effective adaptation strategies is challenging due to the long-term, sequential nature of infrastructure investments, deep climate uncertainty, and the complex interactions between flooding, infrastructure, and mobility impacts. In this work, we propose a novel decision-support framework using reinforcement learning (RL) for long-term flood adaptation planning. Formulated as an integrated assessment model (IAM), the framework combines rainfall projection and flood modeling, transport simulation, and quantification of direct and indirect impacts on infrastructure and mobility. Our RL-based approach learns adaptive strategies that balance investment and maintenance costs against avoided impacts. We evaluate the framework through a case study of Copenhagen's inner city over the 2024-2100 period, testing multiple adaptation options, and different belief and realized climate scenarios. Results show that the framework outperforms traditional optimization approaches by discovering coordinated spatial and temporal adaptation pathways and learning trade-offs between impact reduction and adaptation investment, yielding more resilient strategies. Overall, our results showcase the potential of reinforcement learning as a flexible decision-support tool for adaptive infrastructure planning under climate uncertainty.

</details>


### [224] [The EpisTwin: A Knowledge Graph-Grounded Neuro-Symbolic Architecture for Personal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06290)
*Giovanni Servedio,Potito Aghilar,Alessio Mattiace,Gianni Carmosino,Francesco Musicco,Gabriele Conte,Vito Walter Anelli,Tommaso Di Noia,Francesco Maria Donini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpisTwin是一个神经符号框架，通过构建可验证的个人知识图谱来解决个人AI中的数据孤岛问题，结合图检索增强生成和在线深度视觉优化实现复杂推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个人AI面临用户数据分散在孤立数据孤岛中的问题，而传统的检索增强生成方法依赖非结构化向量相似性，无法捕捉潜在语义拓扑和时间依赖关系，限制了整体意义理解能力。

Method: 1. 使用多模态语言模型将异构跨应用数据转换为语义三元组，构建个人知识图谱；2. 通过代理协调器结合图检索增强生成与在线深度视觉优化，在推理时对个人语义图进行复杂推理；3. 引入PersonalQA-71-100合成基准来评估性能。

Result: EpisTwin在一系列最先进的评估模型上展现出稳健的性能，为可信赖的个人AI提供了有前景的方向。

Conclusion: EpisTwin通过神经符号方法将生成推理建立在可验证的个人知识图谱基础上，为解决个人AI中的数据碎片化和语义理解问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Personal Artificial Intelligence is currently hindered by the fragmentation of user data across isolated silos. While Retrieval-Augmented Generation offers a partial remedy, its reliance on unstructured vector similarity fails to capture the latent semantic topology and temporal dependencies essential for holistic sensemaking. We introduce EpisTwin, a neuro-symbolic framework that grounds generative reasoning in a verifiable, user-centric Personal Knowledge Graph. EpisTwin leverages Multimodal Language Models to lift heterogeneous, cross-application data into semantic triples. At inference, EpisTwin enables complex reasoning over the personal semantic graph via an agentic coordinator that combines Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Online Deep Visual Refinement, dynamically re-grounding symbolic entities in their raw visual context. We also introduce PersonalQA-71-100, a synthetic benchmark designed to simulate a realistic user's digital footprint and evaluate EpisTwin performance. Our framework demonstrates robust results across a suite of state-of-the-art judge models, offering a promising direction for trustworthy Personal AI.

</details>


### [225] [SAHOO: Safeguarded Alignment for High-Order Optimization Objectives in Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06333)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAHOO框架通过三个安全措施监控和控制递归自我改进中的对齐漂移：目标漂移指数、约束保持检查和回归风险量化，在多个任务中实现质量提升同时保持对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进正从理论走向实践，但迭代自我修改可能导致微妙的对齐漂移风险，需要可部署的监控和控制框架来确保对齐保持。

Method: 提出SAHOO框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 目标漂移指数(GDI)，结合语义、词汇、结构和分布度量的多信号检测器；(2) 约束保持检查，强制执行安全关键不变性；(3) 回归风险量化，标记可能撤销先前改进的循环。

Result: 在189个代码生成、数学推理和真实性任务中，SAHOO实现显著质量提升（代码任务18.3%，推理任务16.8%），同时在两个领域保持约束，在真实性任务中保持低违规率。阈值在18个任务的验证集上校准。

Conclusion: SAHOO使递归自我改进期间的对齐保持变得可测量、可部署且可系统验证，揭示了能力-对齐前沿的早期高效改进与后期对齐成本上升的权衡。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement is moving from theory to practice: modern systems can critique, revise, and evaluate their own outputs, yet iterative self-modification risks subtle alignment drift. We introduce SAHOO, a practical framework to monitor and control drift through three safeguards: (i) the Goal Drift Index (GDI), a learned multi-signal detector combining semantic, lexical, structural, and distributional measures; (ii) constraint preservation checks that enforce safety-critical invariants such as syntactic correctness and non-hallucination; and (iii) regression-risk quantification to flag improvement cycles that undo prior gains. Across 189 tasks in code generation, mathematical reasoning, and truthfulness, SAHOO produces substantial quality gains, including 18.3 percent improvement in code tasks and 16.8 percent in reasoning, while preserving constraints in two domains and maintaining low violations in truthfulness. Thresholds are calibrated on a small validation set of 18 tasks across three cycles. We further map the capability-alignment frontier, showing efficient early improvement cycles but rising alignment costs later and exposing domain-specific tensions such as fluency versus factuality. SAHOO therefore makes alignment preservation during recursive self-improvement measurable, deployable, and systematically validated at scale.

</details>


### [226] [Talk Freely, Execute Strictly: Schema-Gated Agentic AI for Flexible and Reproducible Scientific Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06394)
*Joel Strickland,Arjun Vijeta,Chris Moores,Oliwia Bodek,Bogdan Nenchev,Thomas Whitehead,Charles Phillips,Karl Tassenberg,Gareth Conduit,Ben Pellegrini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出schema-gated orchestration架构，通过模式验证在组合工作流层面强制设定执行边界，以解决LLM科学工作流中确定性执行与会话灵活性之间的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可以将研究人员的自然语言目标转化为可执行计算，但科学工作流需要确定性、可追溯性和治理保障，而LLM自主决定运行内容难以保证这些要求。工业研发专家访谈揭示了确定性约束执行与会话灵活性之间的冲突需求。

Method: 提出schema-gated orchestration作为解决方案：模式成为组合工作流层面的强制执行边界，确保所有操作（包括跨步骤依赖）必须通过机器可检查的规范验证才能执行。将需求操作化为执行确定性(ED)和会话灵活性(CF)，使用多模型协议评估20个系统，通过3个LLM家族的15个独立会话进行评分。

Result: 多模型LLM评分显示出高度一致性（ED的Krippendorff α=0.80，CF的α=0.98），表明多模型LLM评分可作为人类专家小组的可重用替代方案。分析揭示了经验帕累托前沿——没有系统同时实现高灵活性和高确定性，但在生成式和以工作流为中心的两个极端之间出现了收敛区域。

Conclusion: schema-gated架构通过分离会话权限和执行权限，能够解耦灵活性与确定性的权衡。提炼出三个操作原则：执行前澄清、约束的计划-行动编排、工具到工作流层面的门控，以指导实际采用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can now translate a researcher's plain-language goal into executable computation, yet scientific workflows demand determinism, provenance, and governance that are difficult to guarantee when an LLM decides what runs. Semi-structured interviews with 18 experts across 10 industrial R&D stakeholders surface 2 competing requirements--deterministic, constrained execution and conversational flexibility without workflow rigidity--together with boundary properties (human-in-the-loop control and transparency) that any resolution must satisfy. We propose schema-gated orchestration as the resolving principle: the schema becomes a mandatory execution boundary at the composed-workflow level, so that nothing runs unless the complete action--including cross-step dependencies--validates against a machine-checkable specification.
  We operationalize the 2 requirements as execution determinism (ED) and conversational flexibility (CF), and use these axes to review 20 systems spanning 5 architectural groups along a validation-scope spectrum. Scores are assigned via a multi-model protocol--15 independent sessions across 3 LLM families--yielding substantial-to-near-perfect inter-model agreement (Krippendorff a=0.80 for ED and a=0.98 for CF), demonstrating that multi-model LLM scoring can serve as a reusable alternative to human expert panels for architectural assessment.
  The resulting landscape reveals an empirical Pareto front--no reviewed system achieves both high flexibility and high determinism--but a convergence zone emerges between the generative and workflow-centric extremes. We argue that a schema-gated architecture, separating conversational from execution authority, is positioned to decouple this trade-off, and distill 3 operational principles--clarification-before-execution, constrained plan-act orchestration, and tool-to-workflow-level gating--to guide adoption.

</details>


### [227] [Boosting deep Reinforcement Learning using pretraining with Logical Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06565)
*Zihan Ye,Phil Chau,Raban Emunds,Jannis Blüml,Cedric Derstroff,Quentin Delfosse,Oleg Arenz,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出H^2RL混合分层强化学习方法，通过符号逻辑选项预训练引导神经网络策略，避免短期奖励陷阱，提升长期决策能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习代理经常出现对齐问题，过度利用早期奖励信号；纯符号方法难以扩展到连续环境；受人类学习新技能能力启发，需要结合符号结构和神经网络的混合方法

Method: 提出H^2RL（混合分层强化学习）框架：1）两阶段方法，将符号结构注入神经强化学习代理；2）逻辑选项预训练策略，引导学习策略远离短期奖励循环；3）最终策略通过标准环境交互进行细化

Result: 实验表明该方法持续改善长期决策能力，代理性能优于强大的神经、符号和神经符号基线方法

Conclusion: H^2RL成功结合符号结构和神经策略的表达能力，解决了深度强化学习的对齐问题，在保持深度策略表达能力的同时改善了长期决策

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning agents are often misaligned, as they over-exploit early reward signals. Recently, several symbolic approaches have addressed these challenges by encoding sparse objectives along with aligned plans. However, purely symbolic architectures are complex to scale and difficult to apply to continuous settings. Hence, we propose a hybrid approach, inspired by humans' ability to acquire new skills. We use a two-stage framework that injects symbolic structure into neural-based reinforcement learning agents without sacrificing the expressivity of deep policies. Our method, called Hybrid Hierarchical RL (H^2RL), introduces a logical option-based pretraining strategy to steer the learning policy away from short-term reward loops and toward goal-directed behavior while allowing the final policy to be refined via standard environment interaction. Empirically, we show that this approach consistently improves long-horizon decision-making and yields agents that outperform strong neural, symbolic, and neuro-symbolic baselines.

</details>
